"C:\\Users\\jelon\\AppData\\Local\\Microsoft\\WindowsApps\\Microsoft.PowerShell_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\pwsh.exe" -Command "Get-Content docs/design/pipeline-architecture.md | Select-String -Pattern '异常|栈|parse|internal|send<|R |cancel|不可变' -Context 2,3; Get-Content lib/src/response.dart -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 10; Get-Content test/rule/transport_rule_test.dart; Get-Content test/unit/key_parity_test.dart" in E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman
 succeeded in 1317ms:

  
  - `Dioman(dio: …, plugins: […])` 产出客户端。一次调用就是一个逻辑请求，穿过 5 个阶段的中间件，最内层调用一次 `dio.fet
ch`。
> - 传输边界把 Dio 的响应或异常判定成 `DiomanResponse`；链路里只流转它，`next()` 不抛错。最外层落定：`get` 成功返回数据
、失败抛出；`send` 返回结果对象；程序 bug 原样抛出原始异常。
  - 插件 = `DiomanPlugin`（名字、阶段、`setup` 或 `handle`）。内置插件位置固定，自定义插件只选阶段。
> - 请求不可变，内层改请求用 `next(patch)`；重试、重放就是再调一次 `next()`。
  - 决策依据见 [0001](../decisions/0001-pipeline-redesign.md)，插件细则见 [插件规格](pipeline-plugin-spec.md)。
  
  ## 1. 结构
    │
    ▼
> ┌─────────────── 落定（内核）：R → data | R | throw R；bug → 原样抛出 ───────────────┐
> │ outer       loading → notify                        每个逻辑请求一次           │
> │ prepare     repath → filter → timeout               请求整形，一次             │
> │ attempt     retry → breaker                         重试边界：往里每次尝试重跑   │
  │ credential  auth                                    每次尝试注入 token          │
  │ source      mock → share → cache → offline          结果来源，可短路            │
> │ ┌──────── 传输（内核）：dio.fetch（使用者的 Dio 拦截器在这里运行）+ 判定 → R ────┐│
  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  ```
  
  | 文件 | 职责 |
  |---|---|
> | `client.dart` | `Dioman`：动词方法、`request/send`、建逻辑请求上下文、登记在途、落定、`use/eject/inspect/cancelAll/
pending/extend/dispose` |
> | `pipeline.dart` | 插件排序（阶段 + 内置位置表）、链编译、`next(patch)`、每层的异常边界 |
  | `stages.dart` | `DiomanStage` 枚举与内置插件位置表 |
> | `context.dart` | `DiomanContext`：请求视图、取消、状态键、`key()`、`fail()`、`markReplayable()`、`wait()`、`cancell
ed()` |
> | `request.dart` | 不可变的 `DiomanRequest`、`RequestPatch`、与 `RequestOptions` 互转 |
  | `transport.dart` | 按需克隆请求体、调用 `dio.fetch`（`validateStatus` 全部放行）、交给工厂判定 |
  | `response.dart` | `DiomanKind`、`DiomanResponse`、`DiomanResponseFactory`、`Envelope` |
  | `options.dart` | 请求级插件选项的统一解析（`resolveOption`） |
  | `token_manager.dart` | `DiomanTokenManager` 接口（去掉 `canRefresh`，见插件规格 auth） |
  
> 删除：`dioman.dart` 里的 `install/installPlugins/DiomanHandle`、`plugin_order.dart`、`replay_support.dart`、`request_
claims.dart`、`extra_keys.dart`、`mock_adapter.dart`，以及 `key/cancel/normalize/log` 四个插件。
  
  ## 2. 一次请求的数据流
  
> 1. 动词方法把 path、query、data、调用级配置交给内核。内核用 `Options.compose(dio.options, path, …)` **合并一次**，得
到有效的 `RequestOptions`，再包成不可变的 `DiomanRequest`（保留有效配置的全部字段）。`dio.fetch` 不会再合并 BaseOptions
（dio 5.11.1 已核对）。
> 2. 内核建逻辑请求级上下文：分配 `id`；建内核 `CancelToken`，并与使用者传入的 token 联动；登记到在途集合（供 `cancelAl
l`、`pending` 使用）。
  3. 按编译好的链从外到内执行。每层拿到的 `ctx.request` 是外层交给它的版本。
> 4. 到达传输层：如果有层调用过 `markReplayable()`，就按类型克隆请求体（见 §8 P4）；把 `DiomanRequest` 转成 `RequestOpt
ions`，设置 `validateStatus: (_) => true` 和内核 `cancelToken`，调用 `dio.fetch`。
> 5. 工厂把响应或异常判定成 `R`，原路返回。每层可以读它、替换它，或者再次调用 `next()`。
> 6. 最外层落定：从在途集合注销、解除取消联动，然后按 §6 返回数据、返回 `R`、抛出 `R`，或抛出原始异常。
  
  ## 3. 阶段与排序
  
  | `prepare` | 请求整形，只跑一次 | 公共参数、签名前的规整 |
  | `attempt` | 重试边界；retry 以内的层每次尝试都重跑 | 每次尝试的日志、请求签名（**自定义插件的默认阶段**） |
> | `credential` | 凭证注入与鉴权失败处理 | 自定义 header 鉴权 |
  | `source` | 结果来源，可以不触网直接返回 | 本地兜底、离线数据 |
  
  规则：
  ```dart
  /// 中间件拿到的上下文；逻辑请求级字段在各层共享，request 是本层视图。
> abstract class DiomanContext<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    int get id;                                   // 逻辑请求序号
    Dioman<R> get client;
>   DiomanRequest get request;                    // 本层看到的请求，不可变
>   CancelToken get cancelToken;                  // 逻辑请求级：使用者 token ∪ cancelAll
>   bool get isCancelled;
>   DiomanLogger get log;                         // 受 debug 开关控制
    String? key();                                // 惰性计算，按 request 缓存；不可哈希或 key:false 时为 null
    T? get<T>(StateKey<T> key);                   // 逻辑请求级状态
    void set<T>(StateKey<T> key, T value);
>   R fail(DiomanFailure init);                   // 凭空造结果，必须经工厂
    void markReplayable();                        // 声明本请求可能被重发，传输层据此克隆请求体
    Future<T?> wait<T>(Future<T> pending, {Duration? timeout});  // 取消原语一，见下
>   R? cancelled();                               // 取消原语二：已取消则返回 cancel 结果
  }
  typedef DiomanNext<R> = Future<R> Function([RequestPatch? patch]);
  final class StateKey<T> { const StateKey(this.description); final String description; }
  ```
  
> **请求不可变**：`DiomanRequest` 的字段都是 final，集合是不可修改的视图。`RequestPatch` 是"要改的字段"加"要删的键"：`h
eaders`、`query`、`extra` 按键合并，`removeHeaders`、`removeQuery` 显式删除；headers 合并不区分大小写；`data`、`path`、
`method`、超时、`cancelToken` 整体替换。同一层两次调用 `next()` 拿到同一个基准请求。
  
  **取消原语**（每个 await 之后都要重查）：
> - `ctx.wait(pending, timeout:)`：等 `pending` 完成、等取消、等超时三者之一。无论哪种结局，都要取消计时器、摘掉对 toke
n 的监听，并忽略迟到的结果。调用时如果已经取消，立即返回 `null`，不挂任何监听。返回 `null` 表示被取消或超时，调用方用 `
ctx.cancelled()` 区分。
> - `ctx.cancelled()`：已取消就返回 `kind: cancel` 的结果，否则返回 `null`。
> - Dio 的 `CancelToken` 没有"摘掉监听"的接口，内核用自己的订阅登记实现 `wait`，保证测试里能断言"没有残留的监听和计时器
"（不变量 I3）。
  
  ## 5. 归一化响应
  
  ```dart
> enum DiomanKind { ok, http, biz, network, timeout, cancel, parse, auth, breaker, offline, internal }
  
  /// 归一化响应：成功、失败、被抛出的都是它。字段在构造时由工厂算好。
  class DiomanResponse<D> implements Exception {
    final DiomanRequest request; // 实际发出的请求；share/cache 时是发出那一次的
    final Response<dynamic>? raw;
>   final Object? error;         // 原始异常
    final StackTrace? errorStack;
    final StackTrace? callSite;  // 仅 debug：发起请求那一刻的调用点
  }
  | `isSuccess(code)` | `code == null \|\| code == 0 \|\| code == '0'` | 业务码判定，只在收到 2xx 响应且是信封时调用 |
  | `logicalStatus(response)` | 失败、2xx、业务码是 400–599 的整数 → 业务码；否则 httpStatus | 使用者可以把 `'TOKEN_EXP
IRED'` 映射成 401 |
> | `expectJson` | `false` | 声明 JSON 却解析失败时判为 `parse` |
  | `create(init)` | `DiomanResponse.new` | 扩展点：返回带额外字段的子类 |
  
  **分类规则**（按优先级）：
  | 输入 | kind | status |
  |---|---|---|
> | 内核 token 已取消，或 `DioExceptionType.cancel` | cancel | 0 |
  | `connectionTimeout` / `sendTimeout` / `receiveTimeout` | timeout | 0 |
  | `connectionError`、`badCertificate`，或 `unknown` 且 `error` 是 `SocketException`/`HttpException`/`TlsException` | 
network | 0 |
  | `badResponse`（只会来自使用者的 Dio 拦截器主动 reject） | http | 取响应状态 |
> | 其余 `DioException.unknown`、非 Dio 异常（使用者拦截器或 Transformer 里的 bug） | internal | 0 |
> | 2xx、`expectJson`、声明 JSON 但数据仍是非空字符串 | parse | httpStatus |
  | 收到响应 | ok / biz / http，按 `isSuccess` 与 2xx 判定 | 按 `logicalStatus` |
  
> 内核负责：工厂自己抛错时，改用内置工厂造 `internal` 结果，并保留第一次的异常，保证外层照常收尾。
  
  ## 6. 落定
  
  | 成功 | `r.data` | `r` |
  | 可预期的失败 | `throw r` | `r`（`ok == false`） |
> | 程序 bug（`kind: internal`） | `Error.throwWithStackTrace(r.error, r.errorStack)` | 同左 |
  
> - **bug 不伪装成请求失败**：中间件、判定类回调、使用者 Dio 拦截器或 Transformer 抛出的异常，链内包成 internal 交给外
层，保证 `next()` 不出错、外层的计数和注销照常完成；落定时抛出原始异常。
  - **调用点**：debug 开启时，内核在发起请求那一刻记下 `StackTrace.current`；失败落定时派生一个带 `callSite` 的副本再抛
，不修改被 share/cache 共享的原对象。生产环境不记录，零开销。
> - **`reject`**：`Dioman(reject: (r) => …)` 在抛出前转换可预期的失败，用来适配需要特定异常类型的生态。默认原样抛出 `r`
。
  
  ## 7. 插件接口
  
  ```dart
> abstract class DiomanPlugin<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    String get name;                              // 内置插件以 dioman: 开头
    DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.attempt; // 自定义插件的默认阶段
    DiomanEnforce get enforce => DiomanEnforce.post;
    DiomanPluginInstance<R> setup(Dioman<R> client);
  }
> abstract class DiomanPluginInstance<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    Future<R> handle(DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next);
    void dispose() {}
  }
  
  ```dart
> class Dioman<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
    Dioman({required Dio dio, List<DiomanPlugin<R>> plugins = const [],
            DiomanResponseFactory<R>? response, DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
>           Object Function(R r)? reject, Object? debug /* bool | DiomanLogger */,
            DiomanClock clock = const DiomanClock.system()});
    Dio get dio;
    Future<T> get<T>(String path, {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call});
    // post / put / patch / delete / head 同形；request<T>(method, path, …)
>   Future<DiomanResponse<T>> send<T>(String method, String path, {…});
    void use(DiomanPlugin<R> plugin);
    bool eject(String name);
    A? plugin<A>();                       // 取插件暴露的操作
    List<(DiomanStage, String)> inspect();
>   int cancelAll([Object? reason]);
    int get pending;
    Dioman<R> extend({BaseOptions? options, List<DiomanPlugin<R>>? plugins});
    void dispose();
  ```
  
> - `DiomanCall`：调用级配置，包含 headers、超时、使用者的 `cancelToken`、`key`、`silent`、以及各插件的请求级选项（以插
件导出的选项键为键）。
  - `extend()` 克隆 Dio 的 BaseOptions，默认继承插件列表并各自重新 `setup`，状态不共享。
> - `dispose()`：先 `cancelAll()`，再按注册的逆序调用各插件实例的 `dispose`（尽力执行完，最后抛出第一个异常）；之后拒绝
新的请求和 `use`。
  
  ## 9. 性能设计
  
  | P4 | 请求体按需克隆：只有 `markReplayable()` 时才克隆。Map/List 递归拷贝，`FormData` 用 `clone()`，`Stream` 等无法
克隆的回退原引用并在 debug 下告警，不让请求失败 |
  | P5 | 传输层 `validateStatus` 全部放行，HTTP 错误不构造 `DioException` |
> | P6 | 失败直接抛 `R`，不另建异常对象；调用点只在 debug 下记录 |
  | P7 | 入口只合并一次配置，传输层直接 `fetch`，没有二次合并 |
  | P8 | 缓存过期惰性删除，写入时按容量节流清扫（按清扫后的条目数，不按固定间隔，避免 axp B6 发现的平方级插入） |
  | P9 | 插件级配置在 `setup` 时预合并；请求级 `true` 或省略时直接复用这份配置 |
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_clock.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_network.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/invariant_harness.dart';

/// 使用真实 Dio 转换器和假 adapter 验证传输边界。
void main() {
  test('[Dioman.cancelAll] 实际 Dio 发送中取消，迟到网络结果不会恢复请求', () async {
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final entered = Completer<void>();
    final network = FakeNetwork(onSend: (_) => entered.complete());
    network.routes['GET:/pending'] = [FakeReply(gate: gate.future)];
    final api = testClient(network: network);
    final pending = api.send<Object?>('GET', '/pending');
    await entered.future;
    expect(network.sends.length, 1);
    expect(api.cancelAll(), 1);
    expect((await pending).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect(api.pending, 0);
    gate.complete();
    await flush();
    expect(api.pending, 0);
  });
  test('[DiomanContext.markReplayable] I7 嵌套请求体经原地修改 Transformer 后每次发送一致',
      () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final clock = FakeClock();
    final harness = InvariantHarness(network, clock);
    final api = testClient(network: network, clock: clock, plugins: [
      ...harness.probes(),
      TestPlugin('replay', handler: (ctx, next) async {
        ctx.markReplayable();
        await next();
        return next();
      }),
    ]);
    api.dio.transformer = _MutatingTransformer();
    final data = {
      'items': [
        {'n': 0}
      ]
    };
    await harness.track(
        1,
        api.post<Object?>('/replay',
            data: data,
            call: const DiomanCall(headers: {invariantRequestHeader: 'one'})));
    expect(network.sends.length, 2);
    expect(network.sends.first.body, {
      'items': [
        {'n': 1}
      ]
    });
    expect(data, {
      'items': [
        {'n': 0}
      ]
    });
    harness.assertAll(api);
  });

  test('[diomanTransport] FormData 克隆、不可克隆 Stream 回退并告警、debug 日志异常隔离',
      () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final logs = <String>[];
    final api = testClient(network: network, debug: logs.add, plugins: [
      TestPlugin('replay', handler: (ctx, next) async {
        ctx.markReplayable();
        await next();
        return next();
      }),
    ]);
    final form = FormData.fromMap({'a': 'b'});
    await api.post<Object?>('/form', data: form);
    expect(form.isFinalized, isFalse);
    expect(network.sends.length, 2);
    expect(logs, isEmpty);
    final stream = Stream<List<int>>.fromIterable([
      [1, 2]
    ]).asBroadcastStream();
    final ctx = testContext(
        request: DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(
            path: '/stream', baseUrl: 'https://example.test', data: stream)),
        log: logs.add);
    ctx.markReplayable();
    await diomanTransport(api.dio, ctx);
    expect(logs, ['请求体无法克隆，回退原引用']);
    final throwLog =
        testClient(debug: (String _) => throw StateError('log'), plugins: [
      TestPlugin('log', handler: (ctx, next) {
        ctx.log('notification');
        return next();
      })
    ]);
    expect(await throwLog.get<String>('/'), 'ok');
  });

  test('[diomanTransport] 取消前不发送、传输 bug 原栈、原始 Dio 拦截器主动 reject', () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final api = testClient(network: network);
    final token = CancelToken()..cancel();
    final ctx = testContext(token: token);
    expect((await diomanTransport(api.dio, ctx)).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect(network.sends, isEmpty);
    final bug = StateError('transformer');
    api.dio.transformer = _ThrowTransformer(bug);
    await expectLater(api.post<Object?>('/bug', data: {}), throwsA(same(bug)));
    expect(api.pending, 0);
    final rejected = testClient();
    rejected.dio.interceptors.add(InterceptorsWrapper(
        onRequest: (o, h) => h.reject(DioException(
            requestOptions: o,
            type: DioExceptionType.badResponse,
            response: Response(requestOptions: o, statusCode: 418)))));
    expect((await rejected.send<Object?>('GET', '/')).status, 418);
  });
}

/// 在嵌套结构上原地改值，避免浅拷贝掩盖重放污染。
class _MutatingTransformer extends SyncTransformer {
  /// 修改后交给 Dio 的真实编码。
  @override
  Future<String> transformRequest(RequestOptions options) async {
    final item = ((options.data as Map)['items'] as List).first as Map;
    item['n'] = (item['n'] as int) + 1;
    return super.transformRequest(options);
  }
}

/// 模拟用户 Transformer 的程序 bug。
class _ThrowTransformer extends SyncTransformer {
  /// 保存原异常。
  _ThrowTransformer(this.bug);

  /// 原始异常对象。
  final Object bug;

  /// 原样抛出。
  @override
  Future<String> transformRequest(RequestOptions options) => throw bug;
}
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dioman/dioman.dart' as legacy;
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

/// 唯一允许同时导入新旧库的差分测试。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanKeyService.build] 固定种子 2400 组与旧 key 逐字节一致', () async {
    final random = Random(20261028);
    Object? value(int depth) {
      if (depth > 2) return random.nextInt(9);
      switch (random.nextInt(6)) {
        case 0:
          return null;
        case 1:
          return random.nextBool();
        case 2:
          return 'v${random.nextInt(30)}';
        case 3:
          return random.nextInt(100);
        case 4:
          return [value(depth + 1), value(depth + 1)];
        default:
          return {'z': value(depth + 1), 'a': value(depth + 1)};
      }
    }

    for (var i = 0; i < 2400; i++) {
      final fast = random.nextBool();
      final requestFast = random.nextBool() ? random.nextBool() : null;
      final ignores = random.nextBool() ? ['ignore'] : <String>[];
      final requestIgnores = random.nextBool() ? ['a'] : null;
      final String? scope = [null, '', 'account:$i'][i % 3];
      final String? explicit =
          i % 7 == 0 ? 'explicit:$i' : (i % 7 == 1 ? '' : null);
      String builder(RequestOptions o) => 'builder:${o.path}';
      String localBuilder(RequestOptions o) => 'request:${o.path}';
      final global = i % 11 == 0 ? builder : null;
      final local = i % 13 == 0 ? localBuilder : null;
      final request = RequestOptions(
          path: '/p${i % 15}?inline=$i',
          baseUrl: 'https://example.test',
          method: i.isEven ? 'GET' : 'POST',
          queryParameters: {'z': i, 'a': '$i', 'ignore': 1},
          data: value(0));
      final old = legacy.DiomanKey(
          fastMode: fast,
          ignores: ignores,
          builder: global,
          namespace: () => scope);
      final options = request.copyWith();
      options.extra[legacy.DiomanKey.pluginName] = legacy.DiomanKeyOptions(
          fastMode: requestFast,
          ignores: requestIgnores,
          key: explicit,
          builder: local);
      final handler = RequestInterceptorHandler();
      old.onRequest(options, handler);
      final next = DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions(
              fastMode: fast,
              ignores: ignores,
              builder: global,
              namespace: () => scope))
          .build(
              request,
              DiomanKeyOptions(
                  fastMode: requestFast,
                  ignores: requestIgnores,
                  key: explicit,
                  builder: local));
      expect(next, options.extra[legacy.kKey], reason: 'seed=20261028 case=$i');
    }
  });

  test('[DiomanKeyService.build] 开关、显式键、异常及不可序列化回退', () {
    final r = RequestOptions(path: '/r', data: {'bad': double.nan});
    const service = DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions());
    expect(service.build(r, false), isNull);
    expect(service.build(r, 'explicit'), 'explicit');
    expect(() => service.build(r, 3), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(service.build(r), contains('NaN'));
    expect(service.build(RequestOptions(data: '')), isNotNull);
    expect(service.build(RequestOptions(data: {})), isNotNull);
    final sorted = RequestOptions(data: {
      'z': {
        'b': 1,
        'a': [2, 1]
      },
      'a': 1
    });
    final reversed = RequestOptions(data: {
      'a': 1,
      'z': {
        'a': [2, 1],
        'b': 1
      }
    });
    expect(service.build(sorted), service.build(reversed));
    expect(
        () => DiomanKeyService(
                DiomanKeyOptions(builder: (_) => throw StateError('builder')))
            .build(r),
        throwsStateError);
    expect(
        () => DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions(
            namespace: () => throw StateError('namespace'))).build(r),
        throwsStateError);
  });
}

import 'client.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';

/// 从同一基准继续执行内层；可多次调用，结果总是响应对象。
typedef DiomanNext<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    = Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> Function([RequestPatch? patch]);

/// 插件只保存配置；每个客户端 setup 一次，状态放在返回实例里。
abstract class DiomanPlugin<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 支持常量插件配置。
  const DiomanPlugin();

  /// 唯一名称；dioman: 前缀保留给内置插件。
  String get name;

  /// 默认位于尝试阶段。
  DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.attempt;

  /// 默认排在阶段内置插件之后。
  DiomanEnforce get enforce => DiomanEnforce.post;

  /// false 时不调用 setup，也不进 inspect。
  bool get enabled => true;

  /// 创建客户端独立状态；null 表示只有 setup 效果，不进入链。
  DiomanPluginInstance<R>? setup(Dioman<R> client);
}

/// 客户端专属插件状态及管理 API 的载体。
abstract class DiomanPluginInstance<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 执行本层，例如 `return next()`；同步和异步异常均由边界隔离。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next);

  /// 释放实例资源，客户端逆注册顺序调用。
  void dispose() {}
}

/// B1 启用 I1/I3/I6/I7；其余接口供后续插件批次接入。
class InvariantHarness {
  /// 绑定网络和时钟。
  InvariantHarness(this.network, this.clock);

  /// 网络发送证据。
  final FakeNetwork network;

  /// 时间资源证据。
  final FakeClock clock;

  /// 外层进入次数。
  final entries = <int, int>{};

  /// 外层离开次数。
  final exits = <int, int>{};

  /// 调用方落定次数。
  final settlements = <int, int>{};

  /// I2 回调配对序列。
  final loading = <Object, List<bool>>{};

  /// I4 每请求通知次数。
  final notifications = <int, int>{};

  /// I5 每轮刷新次数。
  final refreshRounds = <int, int>{};

  /// 各层保存的上下文，用于请求结束后追踪取消订阅。
  final contexts = <DiomanContext<Result>>[];

  /// 生成最外层及每阶段 pre/post 的直通探针。
  List<DiomanPlugin<Result>> probes() => [
        TestPlugin('invariant',
            stage: DiomanStage.outer,
            enforce: DiomanEnforce.pre, handler: (ctx, next) async {
          entries.update(ctx.id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
          contexts.add(ctx);
          final r = await next();
          exits.update(ctx.id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
          return r;
        }),
        for (final stage in DiomanStage.values)
          for (final enforce in DiomanEnforce.values)
            TestPlugin('probe:${stage.name}:${enforce.name}',
                stage: stage, enforce: enforce, handler: (ctx, next) async {
              final r = await next();
              expect(r.kind, isNot(DiomanKind.internal),
                  reason: 'I6 next 不抛且受控流程无 internal');
              return r;
            }),
      ];

  /// 登记一个逻辑调用及其最终一次落定。
  Future<T> track<T>(int id, Future<T> pending) => pending.whenComplete(() {
        settlements.update(id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
      });

  /// 统一断言不变量；测试夹具自身的坏证据也必须被拒绝。
  void assertAll(Dioman<Result> api) {
    expect(entries, isNotEmpty, reason: 'I1 不允许空跑');
    expect(entries.keys.toSet(), exits.keys.toSet());
    expect(entries.keys.toSet(), settlements.keys.toSet());
    for (final id in entries.keys) {
      expect([entries[id], exits[id], settlements[id]], [1, 1, 1]);
    }
    expect(api.pending, 0, reason: 'I3');
    expect(api.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    expect(contexts.every((c) => c.cancellationSubscriptions == 0), isTrue);
    expect(clock.pendingTimers, 0);
    final bodies = <Object?, String>{};
    for (final send in network.sends) {
      if (send.invariant == null) continue;
      final body = jsonEncode(send.body);
      expect(body, bodies.putIfAbsent(send.invariant, () => body),
          reason: 'I7');
    }
    for (final sequence in loading.values) {
      expect(sequence.length.isEven, isTrue);
      for (var i = 0; i < sequence.length; i++) {
        expect(sequence[i], i.isEven);
      }
    }
    expect(notifications.values.every((n) => n <= 1), isTrue);
    expect(refreshRounds.values.every((n) => n == 1), isTrue);
  }
}

/// 独立洋葱管线入口；与旧版 dioman.dart 无互相依赖。
library;

export 'src/pipeline/client.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/clock.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/context.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/key.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/options.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/pipeline.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/plugin.dart';
export 'src/pipeline/request.dart';

### client.dart
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'clock.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'key.dart';
import 'options.dart';
import 'pipeline.dart';
import 'plugin.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';
import 'transport.dart';

/// 关闭 debug 时复用的空日志器。
void _noLog(String _) {}

/// 以 Dio 为引擎的洋葱管线客户端；旧入口与本库完全独立。
class Dioman<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 创建客户端，例如 `Dioman(dio: Dio(), debug: true)`。
  /// [response] 控制归一化，[reject] 只转换动词方法的可预期失败。
  Dioman(
      {required this.dio,
      List<DiomanPlugin<R>> plugins = const [],
      DiomanResponseFactory<R>? response,
      this.key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
      this.reject,
      Object? debug,
      this.clock = const DiomanClock.system()})
      : response = response ?? DiomanResponseFactory<R>(),
        _debug = debug != null && debug != false,
        _logger =
            debug is DiomanLogger ? debug : (debug == true ? print : _noLog) {
    if (debug != null && debug is! bool && debug is! DiomanLogger) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(debug, 'debug');
    }
    validateDiomanPlugins(plugins);
    try {
      for (final plugin in plugins) {
        _registrations.add(DiomanRegistration(
            plugin, plugin.enabled ? plugin.setup(this) : null));
      }
      _compile();
    } catch (_) {
      for (final registration in _registrations.reversed) {
        try {
          registration.instance?.dispose();
        } catch (_) {/* 保留安装时首次异常。 */}
      }
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  /// 底层 Dio；用户拦截器在每次传输时运行。
  final Dio dio;

  /// 客户端的归一化服务。
  final DiomanResponseFactory<R> response;

  /// 客户端构键配置。
  final DiomanKeyOptions key;

  /// 可预期失败的最终异常转换器。
  final Object Function(DiomanResponse<dynamic>)? reject;

  /// 可注入时间服务。
  final DiomanClock clock;

  /// 是否记录调用点。
  final bool _debug;

  /// 用户日志器或默认输出。
  final DiomanLogger _logger;

  /// 注册顺序，供生命周期和 extend 使用。
  final _registrations = <DiomanRegistration<R>>[];

  /// 已排序的执行层快照。
  List<DiomanRegistration<R>> _layers = [];

  /// 编译后的链，在途请求捕获当前值。
  late DiomanRunner<R> _run;

  /// 活跃逻辑请求。
  final _pending = <DiomanContextState>{};

  /// 请求序号。
  int _sequence = 0;

  /// 生命周期状态。
  bool _disposed = false;

  /// 当前活跃逻辑请求数。
  int get pending => _pending.length;

  /// 活跃的内核取消等待订阅总数，供不变量检查。
  int get cancellationSubscriptions =>
      _pending.fold(0, (n, s) => n + s.listeners.length);

  /// 隔离日志回调异常，避免 debug 改变请求结果。
  void _log(String message) {
    try {
      _logger(message);
    } catch (_) {/* 日志通知不影响请求。 */}
  }

  /// 拒绝销毁后的操作。
  void _checkAlive() {
    if (_disposed) throw StateError('客户端已销毁');
  }

  /// 在配置变动时编译链，不在请求期间排序。
  void _compile() {
    _layers = sortDiomanPipeline(_registrations);
    _run = compileDiomanPipeline(_layers, (ctx) => diomanTransport(dio, ctx));
  }

  /// 添加插件；先校验再 setup，安装失败不修改当前链。
  void use(DiomanPlugin<R> plugin) {
    _checkAlive();
    validateDiomanPlugins([..._registrations.map((r) => r.plugin), plugin]);
    final instance = plugin.enabled ? plugin.setup(this) : null;
    _registrations.add(DiomanRegistration(plugin, instance));
    _compile();
  }

  /// 按 [name] 移除并释放插件；不存在返回 false，在途保留原链。
  bool eject(String name) {
    final index = _registrations.indexWhere((r) => r.plugin.name == name);
    if (index < 0) return false;
    final removed = _registrations.removeAt(index);
    _compile();
    removed.instance?.dispose();
    return true;
  }

  /// 返回实现 [A] 的第一个实例，例如 `api.plugin<CacheApi>()`。
  A? plugin<A>() {
    for (final r in _registrations) {
      if (r.instance is A) return r.instance as A;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// 返回解析后的固定阶段和名称顺序，返回列表不可修改。
  List<(DiomanStage, String)> inspect() => List.unmodifiable(_layers.map((r) {
        final fixed = diomanBuiltinStages.entries
            .where((e) => e.value.contains(r.plugin.name));
        return (
          fixed.isEmpty ? r.plugin.stage : fixed.single.key,
          r.plugin.name
        );
      }));

  /// 中止尚未取消的逻辑请求，返回本次中止数。
  int cancelAll([Object? reason]) {
    var count = 0;
    for (final state in _pending.toList()) {
      if (!state.token.isCancelled) {
        count++;
        state.cancel(reason);
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  /// 克隆 BaseOptions 并重新 setup 插件，状态不共享；[plugins] 替换继承列表。
  Dioman<R> extend({BaseOptions? options, List<DiomanPlugin<R>>? plugins}) {
    _checkAlive();
    final base = (options ?? dio.options).copyWith();
    base.headers = _cloneMap(base.headers);
    base.queryParameters = _cloneMap(base.queryParameters);
    base.extra = _cloneMap(base.extra);
    return Dioman<R>(
        dio: Dio(base),
        plugins: plugins ?? _registrations.map((r) => r.plugin).toList(),
        response: response,
        key: key,
        reject: reject,
        debug: _debug ? _logger : false,
        clock: clock);
  }

  /// 先取消请求，再逆序释放全部实例；清理完毕后原栈抛出首个异常。
  void dispose() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _disposed = true;
    cancelAll();
    final errors = <(Object, StackTrace)>[];
    for (final r in _registrations.reversed) {
      try {
        r.instance?.dispose();
      } catch (e, s) {
        errors.add((e, s));
      }
    }
    if (errors.isNotEmpty) {
      Error.throwWithStackTrace(errors.first.$1, errors.first.$2);
    }
  }

  /// 执行请求并返回结果；可预期失败不抛，internal 原异常原栈抛出。
  /// 例如 `await api.send<Object?>('GET', '/pets')`。
  Future<DiomanResponse<T>> send<T>(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    final result =
        await _dispatch(method, path, query: query, data: data, call: call);
    return result.copy<T>();
  }

  /// 执行任意 [method] 并返回数据；可预期失败经 reject 后抛出。
  Future<T> request<T>(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    final result =
        await _dispatch(method, path, query: query, data: data, call: call);
    if (!result.ok) throw (reject?.call(result) ?? result);
    return result.data as T;
  }

  /// 发送 GET 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.get<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> get<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('GET', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 POST 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.post<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> post<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('POST', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 PUT 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.put<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> put<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('PUT', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 PATCH 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.patch<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> patch<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('PATCH', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 DELETE 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.delete<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> delete<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('DELETE', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 HEAD 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.head<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> head<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('HEAD', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 入口仅 compose 一次；登记后所有可能抛错的构造都在 finally 保护内。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> _dispatch(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    _checkAlive();
    final run = _run;
    final site = _debug ? StackTrace.current : null;
    final state = DiomanContextState(CancelToken());
    DiomanCancelLink? userLink;
    _pending.add(state);
    try {
      final c = call ?? const DiomanCall();
      if (c.cancelToken != null) {
        userLink = DiomanCancelLink(c.cancelToken!, state.cancel);
      }
      final options = Options(
              method: method,
              headers: c.headers,
              sendTimeout: c.sendTimeout,
              receiveTimeout: c.receiveTimeout)
          .compose(dio.options, path, queryParameters: query, data: data);
      if (c.connectTimeout != null) options.connectTimeout = c.connectTimeout;
      final ctx = DiomanContext<R>(
          id: ++_sequence,
          client: this,
          request: DiomanRequest(options, call: c),
          state: state,
          factory: response,
          keyService: DiomanKeyService(key),
          clock: clock,
          log: _log);
      var result = await run(ctx);
      result = ctx.cancelled() ?? result;
      if (!result.ok && site != null && result.kind != DiomanKind.internal) {
        result = response.withCallSite(result, site);
      }
      if (result.kind == DiomanKind.internal) {
        Error.throwWithStackTrace(result.error!, result.errorStack!);
      }
      return result;
    } finally {
      userLink?.dispose();
      state.dispose();
      _pending.remove(state);
    }
  }
}

/// 深拷贝 BaseOptions 的 JSON 容器，避免 extend 修改父客户端。
Map<String, dynamic> _cloneMap(Map<String, dynamic> value) =>
    value.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, _cloneValue(v)));

/// 递归复制配置值，非集合保持身份。
Object? _cloneValue(Object? value) {
  if (value is Map) return value.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, _cloneValue(v)));
  if (value is List) return value.map(_cloneValue).toList();
  return value;
}

### clock.dart
import 'dart:async';

/// 可注入的时间服务；测试可实现本接口并推进时间。
abstract class DiomanClock {
  /// 使用系统时钟，例如 `const DiomanClock.system()`。
  const factory DiomanClock.system() = _SystemClock;

  /// 返回当前时间。
  DateTime now();

  /// 在 [duration] 后调用 [callback]，返回可取消的计时器。
  Timer createTimer(Duration duration, void Function() callback);
}

/// 系统时间与可取消计时器的实现。
class _SystemClock implements DiomanClock {
  /// 创建无状态时钟。
  const _SystemClock();

  /// 返回系统时间。
  @override
  DateTime now() => DateTime.now();

  /// 创建单次计时器。
  @override
  Timer createTimer(Duration duration, void Function() callback) =>
      Timer(duration, callback);
}

### context.dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'client.dart';
import 'clock.dart';
import 'key.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';

/// debug 日志回调；关闭 debug 时不会调用。
typedef DiomanLogger = void Function(String message);

/// 逻辑请求状态键；使用非 const 实例保证按身份隔离。
class StateKey<T> {
  /// 创建键，例如 `final attempt = StateKey<int>('attempt')`。
  StateKey(this.description);

  /// 调试说明。
  final String description;
}

/// 可解除目标的 Dio 取消桥；Dio Future 自身没有退订接口。
class DiomanCancelLink {
  /// 将 [source] 联动到 [target]，已取消时同步传递。
  DiomanCancelLink(CancelToken source, void Function(Object?) target)
      : _target = target {
    if (source.isCancelled) {
      target(source.cancelError);
    } else {
      source.whenCancel.then(_deliver);
    }
  }

  /// 可清除的订阅目标，清理后不再引用请求状态。
  void Function(Object?)? _target;

  /// 转发用户取消。
  void _deliver(Object? reason) => _target?.call(reason);

  /// 解除逻辑订阅；迟到的取消不再触及请求。
  void dispose() => _target = null;
}

/// 一个逻辑请求的共享状态和取消订阅登记。
class DiomanContextState {
  /// 创建状态并监听内核令牌；测试也可独立构造上下文。
  DiomanContextState(this.token) {
    _link = DiomanCancelLink(token, (_) => _notify());
  }

  /// 内核逻辑取消令牌。
  final CancelToken token;

  /// Dio 到内核订阅表的桥。
  late final DiomanCancelLink _link;

  /// 惰性分配的类型化值。
  Map<Object, Object?>? values;

  /// 按请求身份缓存 key。
  final keys = Map<DiomanRequest, String?>.identity();

  /// 等待取消的订阅表。
  final listeners = <void Function(), CancelToken>{};

  /// 是否需要克隆发送体。
  bool replayable = false;

  /// 通知副本，允许回调在执行中退订。
  void _notify() {
    for (final entry in listeners.entries.toList()) {
      if (identical(entry.value, token)) entry.key();
    }
  }

  /// 同步取消内核请求并通知等待者。
  void cancel([Object? reason]) {
    token.cancel(reason);
    _notify();
  }

  /// 释放桥并结束剩余等待。
  void dispose() {
    _link.dispose();
    for (final listener in listeners.keys.toList()) {
      listener();
    }
    listeners.clear();
  }
}

/// 每层的固定形状上下文；派生请求共享逻辑状态。
class DiomanContext<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 注入内核服务；例如测试可直接构造而不发起请求。
  DiomanContext(
      {required this.id,
      required this.client,
      required this.request,
      required this.state,
      required this.factory,
      required this.keyService,
      required this.clock,
      required this.log});

  /// 请求序号。
  final int id;

  /// 所属客户端。
  final Dioman<R> client;

  /// 本层请求视图。
  final DiomanRequest request;

  /// 共享状态。
  final DiomanContextState state;

  /// 结果服务。
  final DiomanResponseFactory<R> factory;

  /// 构键服务。
  final DiomanKeyService keyService;

  /// 注入时钟。
  final DiomanClock clock;

  /// 受 debug 开关控制的日志器。
  final DiomanLogger log;

  /// 当前传输的取消令牌，补丁可以覆盖。
  CancelToken get cancelToken => request.cancelToken ?? state.token;

  /// 逻辑请求或当前传输是否取消。
  bool get isCancelled => cancelToken.isCancelled;

  /// 当前逻辑请求残留的等待订阅数。
  int get cancellationSubscriptions => state.listeners.length;

  /// 是否保护重放请求体。
  bool get replayable => state.replayable;

  /// 惰性构键并按请求身份缓存；false 选项返回 null。
  String? key() {
    if (!state.keys.containsKey(request)) {
      state.keys[request] =
          keyService.build(request.toRequestOptions(), request.call.key);
    }
    return state.keys[request];
  }

  /// 读取 [key] 的类型化值，未设置返回 null。
  T? get<T>(StateKey<T> key) => state.values?[key] as T?;

  /// 写入 [key] 的类型化 [value]，各层共享。
  void set<T>(StateKey<T> key, T value) => (state.values ??= {})[key] = value;

  /// 经工厂生成结果；坏扩展工厂回退基础 internal，以保证 next 不抛错。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> fail(DiomanFailure failure) =>
      factory.fail(request, failure);

  /// 声明可能重放；后续每次发送均独立克隆请求体。
  void markReplayable() => state.replayable = true;

  /// 已取消则造 cancel 结果，否则返回 null；每次 await 后应调用。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic>? cancelled() =>
      isCancelled ? fail(const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.cancel)) : null;

  /// 派生请求视图，保留所有逻辑服务与共享状态。
  DiomanContext<R> derive(RequestPatch patch) => DiomanContext(
      id: id,
      client: client,
      request: request.patch(patch),
      state: state,
      factory: factory,
      keyService: keyService,
      clock: clock,
      log: log);

  /// 等待 [pending]、取消或 [timeout]；后两者返回 null，结束后无订阅及计时器。
  /// 已取消立即返回；迟到的值或异常被消费且不会改变结局。
  Future<T?> wait<T>(Future<T> pending, {Duration? timeout}) {
    final result = Completer<T?>();
    Timer? timer;
    DiomanCancelLink? localLink;
    late void Function() cancel;
    void clean() {
      timer?.cancel();
      localLink?.dispose();
      state.listeners.remove(cancel);
    }

    void finish(T? value) {
      if (result.isCompleted) return;
      clean();
      result.complete(value);
    }

    cancel = () => finish(null);
    // 即使已经取消，也消费输入的迟到异常。
    pending.then((value) => finish(value),
        onError: (Object error, StackTrace stack) {
      if (result.isCompleted) return;
      clean();
      result.completeError(error, stack);
    });
    if (isCancelled) {
      finish(null);
      return result.future;
    }
    state.listeners[cancel] = cancelToken;
    if (!identical(cancelToken, state.token)) {
      localLink = DiomanCancelLink(cancelToken, (_) => cancel());
    }
    if (timeout != null) timer = clock.createTimer(timeout, cancel);
    return result.future;
  }
}

### key.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 客户端和请求级的构键配置；空字段继承客户端值。
class DiomanKeyOptions {
  /// 创建配置，例如 `DiomanKeyOptions(fastMode: true)`。
  const DiomanKeyOptions(
      {this.fastMode, this.ignores, this.builder, this.namespace, this.key});

  /// 快速模式只编码方法和路径。
  final bool? fastMode;

  /// 与客户端取并集的忽略字段。
  final List<String>? ignores;

  /// 自定义构键器；抛错按程序异常处理。
  final String Function(RequestOptions)? builder;

  /// 客户端账号作用域，不应返回敏感令牌。
  final String? Function()? namespace;

  /// 请求级显式键；空串沿用自动计算。
  final String? key;
}

/// 与旧版算法独立共存的构键服务。
class DiomanKeyService {
  /// 创建服务，例如 `DiomanKeyService(const DiomanKeyOptions())`。
  const DiomanKeyService(this.options);

  /// 客户端构键配置。
  final DiomanKeyOptions options;

  /// 按请求 builder、请求 key、客户端 builder、计算的次序返回键；false 返回 null。
  String? build(RequestOptions request, [Object? override]) {
    if (override == false) return null;
    if (override != null &&
        override is! String &&
        override is! DiomanKeyOptions) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(override, 'key');
    }
    final o = override is DiomanKeyOptions ? override : null;
    final scope = options.namespace?.call();
    final explicit = override is String ? override : o?.key;
    if (o?.builder != null) return _scopedKey(o!.builder!(request), scope);
    if (explicit != null && explicit.isNotEmpty) {
      return _scopedKey(explicit, scope);
    }
    if (options.builder != null) {
      return _scopedKey(options.builder!(request), scope);
    }
    final ignores = _merge(options.ignores ?? const [], o?.ignores);
    return (o?.fastMode ?? options.fastMode ?? false)
        ? _scopedKey(_fastKey(request), scope)
        : _deepKey(request, ignores, scope);
  }

  /// 为快速键或自定义键增加独立作用域字段，空作用域保持原键。
  static String _scopedKey(String key, String? scope) =>
      scope == null || scope.isEmpty ? key : jsonEncode([key, scope]);

  /// Merges [override] into [base] (union), or returns [base] unchanged when
  /// [override] is `null`.
  ///
  /// 把[override]与[base]取并集合并；[override]为`null`时原样返回[base]。
  static List<String> _merge(List<String> base, List<String>? override) =>
      override == null ? base : {...base, ...override}.toList();

  /// 使用方法、来源和路径生成快速键。
  String _fastKey(RequestOptions o) =>
      '${o.method.toUpperCase()}:${o.uri.hasAuthority ? o.uri.origin : ''}${o.uri.path}';

  /// 分字段编码规范化查询及请求体，保留非结构化对象的身份。
  String _deepKey(RequestOptions o, List<String> ignoreKeys, String? scope) {
    final uri = o.uri;
    final params = <String, dynamic>{
      for (final e in uri.queryParametersAll.entries)
        if (!ignoreKeys.contains(e.key))
          e.key: e.value.length == 1 ? e.value.single : e.value,
    };
    final data = o.data;
    Object? body;
    if (data is Map) {
      final filtered = <String, dynamic>{
        for (final e in data.entries)
          if (!ignoreKeys.contains('${e.key}')) '${e.key}': e.value,
      };
      if (filtered.isNotEmpty) body = {'map': _encode(filtered)};
    } else if (data is String && data.isNotEmpty) {
      body = {'string': data};
    } else if (data != null) {
      body = {'identity': identityHashCode(data)};
    }
    return jsonEncode([
      _fastKey(o),
      _encode(params),
      body,
      if (scope != null && scope.isNotEmpty) scope,
    ]);
  }

  /// 递归排序Map字段并保留List顺序后编码，无法序列化时保留原Map的字符串表示。
  static String _encode(Map v) {
    try {
      return jsonEncode(_normalize(v));
    } catch (_) {
      return v.toString();
    }
  }

  /// 返回递归规范化的副本：Map键按字符串比较排序，List保序，其他值原样保留。
  static Object? _normalize(Object? v) {
    if (v is Map) {
      final entries = v.entries.toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => '${a.key}'.compareTo('${b.key}'));
      return Map.fromEntries(
        entries.map((e) => MapEntry(e.key, _normalize(e.value))),
      );
    }
    if (v is List) return v.map(_normalize).toList();
    return v;
  }
}

### options.dart
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 插件请求级选项键；按对象身份隔离，建议使用静态 final 实例。
class DiomanOptionKey<T> {
  /// [description] 仅供调试，例如 `DiomanOptionKey<int>('retry')`。
  DiomanOptionKey(this.description);

  /// 调试说明。
  final String description;
}

/// 解析统一选项；省略/true 复用 [base]，false 关闭；[optIn] 控制省略时关闭。
/// [merge] 合并类型化覆盖，[shorthand] 返回简写解析值；未知类型抛 ArgumentError。
/// 示例：`resolveOption<int>(true, base: 3, merge: (a, b) => b)`。
T? resolveOption<T>(Object? value,
    {required T base,
    required T Function(T base, T override) merge,
    T? Function(Object value)? shorthand,
    bool optIn = false}) {
  if (value == false || (value == null && optIn)) return null;
  if (value == null || value == true) return base;
  if (value is T) return merge(base, value as T);
  final short = shorthand?.call(value);
  if (short != null) return merge(base, short);
  throw ArgumentError.value(value, 'value', '不支持的插件选项');
}

/// 一次逻辑调用的配置；省略字段沿用 Dio 或客户端配置。
class DiomanCall {
  /// 创建调用配置，例如 `DiomanCall(key: false, envelope: false)`。
  const DiomanCall(
      {this.headers,
      this.connectTimeout,
      this.sendTimeout,
      this.receiveTimeout,
      this.cancelToken,
      this.key,
      this.envelope,
      this.silent = false,
      this.options = const {}});

  /// 按不区分大小写的键覆盖请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic>? headers;

  /// 连接超时。
  final Duration? connectTimeout;

  /// 发送超时。
  final Duration? sendTimeout;

  /// 接收超时。
  final Duration? receiveTimeout;

  /// 用户取消令牌。
  final CancelToken? cancelToken;

  /// String、false 或 DiomanKeyOptions；null 为自动计算。
  final Object? key;

  /// false 禁用本次信封识别。
  final bool? envelope;

  /// 是否静默通知。
  final bool silent;

  /// 插件的类型化请求级选项。
  final Map<DiomanOptionKey, Object?> options;
}

### pipeline.dart
import 'context.dart';
import 'plugin.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';

/// 编译后的上下文执行器。
typedef DiomanRunner<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    = Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> Function(DiomanContext<R> ctx);

/// 已安装的配置及实例。
class DiomanRegistration<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 保存配置与客户端实例。
  const DiomanRegistration(this.plugin, this.instance);

  /// 插件配置。
  final DiomanPlugin<R> plugin;

  /// setup 返回的独立状态。
  final DiomanPluginInstance<R>? instance;
}

/// 按名称确定位置；自定义插件位于同阶段内置插件前后。
(int, int) _position(DiomanPlugin plugin) {
  for (final entry in diomanBuiltinStages.entries) {
    final index = entry.value.indexOf(plugin.name);
    if (index >= 0) return (entry.key.index, index);
  }
  if (plugin.name.startsWith('dioman:')) {
    throw ArgumentError.value(plugin.name, 'name', '保留前缀');
  }
  return (plugin.stage.index, plugin.enforce == DiomanEnforce.pre ? -1 : 100);
}

/// 在 setup 前验证重名和保留前缀，拒绝非法配置且不产生副作用。
void validateDiomanPlugins(Iterable<DiomanPlugin> plugins) {
  final names = <String>{};
  for (final plugin in plugins) {
    if (!names.add(plugin.name)) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(plugin.name, 'name', '插件重名');
    }
    _position(plugin);
  }
}

/// 稳定排序已启用且有 handle 的插件；不修改输入。
List<DiomanRegistration<R>>
    sortDiomanPipeline<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
        List<DiomanRegistration<R>> registrations) {
  final indexed =
      registrations.indexed.where((e) => e.$2.instance != null).toList();
  indexed.sort((a, b) {
    final x = _position(a.$2.plugin);
    final y = _position(b.$2.plugin);
    final stage = x.$1.compareTo(y.$1);
    if (stage != 0) return stage;
    final order = x.$2.compareTo(y.$2);
    return order == 0 ? a.$1.compareTo(b.$1) : order;
  });
  return indexed.map((e) => e.$2).toList();
}

/// 从内到外编译一次；每层含一个异常边界，无补丁复用上下文。
DiomanRunner<R> compileDiomanPipeline<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
    List<DiomanRegistration<R>> layers, DiomanRunner<R> transport) {
  var run = transport;
  for (final layer in layers.reversed) {
    final inner = run;
    run = (ctx) async {
      try {
        return await layer.instance!.handle(ctx, ([patch]) async {
          DiomanContext<R> target;
          try {
            target = patch == null ? ctx : ctx.derive(patch);
          } catch (e, s) {
            return ctx.fail(DiomanFailure(
                kind: DiomanKind.internal, error: e, errorStack: s));
          }
          return inner(target);
        });
      } catch (e, s) {
        return ctx.fail(
            DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.internal, error: e, errorStack: s));
      }
    };
  }
  return run;
}

### plugin.dart
import 'client.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';

/// 从同一基准继续执行内层；可多次调用，结果总是响应对象。
typedef DiomanNext<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    = Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> Function([RequestPatch? patch]);

/// 插件只保存配置；每个客户端 setup 一次，状态放在返回实例里。
abstract class DiomanPlugin<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 支持常量插件配置。
  const DiomanPlugin();

  /// 唯一名称；dioman: 前缀保留给内置插件。
  String get name;

  /// 默认位于尝试阶段。
  DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.attempt;

  /// 默认排在阶段内置插件之后。
  DiomanEnforce get enforce => DiomanEnforce.post;

  /// false 时不调用 setup，也不进 inspect。
  bool get enabled => true;

  /// 创建客户端独立状态；null 表示只有 setup 效果，不进入链。
  DiomanPluginInstance<R>? setup(Dioman<R> client);
}

/// 客户端专属插件状态及管理 API 的载体。
abstract class DiomanPluginInstance<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 执行本层，例如 `return next()`；同步和异步异常均由边界隔离。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next);

  /// 释放实例资源，客户端逆注册顺序调用。
  void dispose() {}
}

### request.dart
import 'dart:collection';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'options.dart';

/// 区分未提供和显式 null 的补丁哨兵。
const _absent = Object();

/// 有效 Dio 配置的不可变请求视图；入口合并一次后构造。
class DiomanRequest {
  /// 从 [options] 拷贝有效配置；[call] 保存调用选项，例如 `DiomanRequest(ro)`。
  DiomanRequest(RequestOptions options, {DiomanCall call = const DiomanCall()})
      : _options = options.copyWith(),
        data = options.data,
        headers = Map.unmodifiable(options.headers),
        query = Map.unmodifiable(options.queryParameters),
        extra = Map.unmodifiable(options.extra),
        call = DiomanCall(
            key: call.key,
            envelope: call.envelope,
            silent: call.silent,
            options: Map.unmodifiable(call.options));

  /// 完整有效配置的私有副本，永不交给 Dio 修改。
  final RequestOptions _options;

  /// 请求体引用；不透明载荷由传输按需克隆，插件通过补丁替换。
  final Object? data;

  /// 不可修改的请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic> headers;

  /// 不可修改的查询参数。
  final Map<String, dynamic> query;

  /// 不可修改的用户扩展字段。
  final Map<String, dynamic> extra;

  /// 调用配置。
  final DiomanCall call;

  /// 有效路径。
  String get path => _options.path;

  /// 有效方法。
  String get method => _options.method;

  /// 有效基础地址。
  String get baseUrl => _options.baseUrl;

  /// 有效 URI。
  Uri get uri => toRequestOptions().uri;

  /// 连接超时。
  Duration? get connectTimeout => _options.connectTimeout;

  /// 发送超时。
  Duration? get sendTimeout => _options.sendTimeout;

  /// 接收超时。
  Duration? get receiveTimeout => _options.receiveTimeout;

  /// 当前传输令牌；未指定时内核使用逻辑请求令牌。
  CancelToken? get cancelToken => _options.cancelToken;

  /// 通知静默标记。
  bool get silent => call.silent;

  /// 返回可独立修改的有效 Dio 配置，例如 `dio.fetch(r.toRequestOptions())`。
  RequestOptions toRequestOptions() => _options.copyWith(
      headers: Map.from(headers),
      queryParameters: Map.from(query),
      extra: Map.from(extra))
    ..data = data;

  /// 合并 [patch] 并返回独立视图；不修改基准请求。
  DiomanRequest patch(RequestPatch patch) {
    final o = toRequestOptions();
    final h = LinkedHashMap<String, dynamic>.from(o.headers);
    for (final key in patch.removeHeaders) {
      h.removeWhere((k, _) => k.toLowerCase() == key.toLowerCase());
    }
    for (final e in patch.headers.entries) {
      h.removeWhere((k, _) => k.toLowerCase() == e.key.toLowerCase());
      h[e.key] = e.value;
    }
    o.headers = h;
    o.queryParameters.removeWhere((k, _) => patch.removeQuery.contains(k));
    o.queryParameters.addAll(patch.query);
    o.extra.addAll(patch.extra);
    if (!identical(patch.data, _absent)) o.data = patch.data;
    if (patch.path != null) o.path = patch.path!;
    if (patch.method != null) o.method = patch.method!;
    if (!identical(patch.connectTimeout, _absent)) {
      o.connectTimeout = patch.connectTimeout as Duration?;
    }
    if (!identical(patch.sendTimeout, _absent)) {
      o.sendTimeout = patch.sendTimeout as Duration?;
    }
    if (!identical(patch.receiveTimeout, _absent)) {
      o.receiveTimeout = patch.receiveTimeout as Duration?;
    }
    if (!identical(patch.cancelToken, _absent)) {
      o.cancelToken = patch.cancelToken as CancelToken?;
    }
    return DiomanRequest(o, call: call);
  }
}

/// 内层请求的改动；集合按键合并，删除必须显式提供删除集合。
class RequestPatch {
  /// 创建补丁，例如 `RequestPatch(data: null, removeHeaders: {'Authorization'})`。
  const RequestPatch(
      {this.headers = const {},
      this.query = const {},
      this.extra = const {},
      this.removeHeaders = const {},
      this.removeQuery = const {},
      this.data = _absent,
      this.path,
      this.method,
      this.connectTimeout = _absent,
      this.sendTimeout = _absent,
      this.receiveTimeout = _absent,
      this.cancelToken = _absent});

  /// 合并的请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic> headers;

  /// 合并的查询参数。
  final Map<String, dynamic> query;

  /// 合并的用户扩展。
  final Map<String, dynamic> extra;

  /// 不区分大小写的待删除请求头。
  final Set<String> removeHeaders;

  /// 待删除查询键。
  final Set<String> removeQuery;

  /// 整体替换的请求体，允许 null。
  final Object? data;

  /// 替换路径。
  final String? path;

  /// 替换方法。
  final String? method;

  /// 替换连接超时，允许 null。
  final Object? connectTimeout;

  /// 替换发送超时，允许 null。
  final Object? sendTimeout;

  /// 替换接收超时，允许 null。
  final Object? receiveTimeout;

  /// 替换传输取消令牌，允许 null。
  final Object? cancelToken;
}

### response.dart
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'request.dart';

/// 链内统一使用的结果类别。
enum DiomanKind {
  ok,
  http,
  biz,
  network,
  timeout,
  cancel,
  parse,
  auth,
  breaker,
  offline,
  internal
}

/// 业务信封字段；含 code 且含 data/message 之一才默认识别。
class Envelope {
  /// 定制字段，例如 `Envelope(code: 'status')`。
  const Envelope(
      {this.code = 'code', this.data = 'data', this.message = 'message'});

  /// 业务码字段。
  final String code;

  /// 数据字段。
  final String data;

  /// 消息字段。
  final String message;
}

/// 不可变归一化结果，同时是可预期失败的异常载体。
class DiomanResponse<D> implements Exception {
  /// 创建已分类的结果；插件通常通过 ctx.fail 构造。
  const DiomanResponse(
      {required this.ok,
      required this.kind,
      required this.status,
      required this.httpStatus,
      this.code,
      this.message = '',
      required this.data,
      this.reason,
      required this.request,
      this.raw,
      this.error,
      this.errorStack,
      this.callSite});

  /// 是否成功。
  final bool ok;

  /// 结果类别。
  final DiomanKind kind;

  /// 插件读取的逻辑状态。
  final int status;

  /// 原始 HTTP 状态，未收到为零。
  final int httpStatus;

  /// 原始业务码。
  final Object? code;

  /// 结果消息。
  final String message;

  /// 已拆包的数据。
  final D? data;

  /// 插件失败的细分原因。
  final String? reason;

  /// 产生此结果的请求。
  final DiomanRequest request;

  /// Dio 原始响应。
  final Response<dynamic>? raw;

  /// 首次程序异常或传输异常。
  final Object? error;

  /// 原始异常栈。
  final StackTrace? errorStack;

  /// debug 模式下的调用点。
  final StackTrace? callSite;

  /// 派生指定数据类型及调用点；原对象不会被修改。
  DiomanResponse<T> copy<T>({StackTrace? callSite}) => DiomanResponse<T>(
      ok: ok,
      kind: kind,
      status: status,
      httpStatus: httpStatus,
      code: code,
      message: message,
      data: ok ? data as T? : (data is T ? data as T : null),
      reason: reason,
      request: request,
      raw: raw,
      error: error,
      errorStack: errorStack,
      callSite: callSite ?? this.callSite);

  /// 安全描述只包含标量，避免 raw、data 或 error 的循环引用及自定义 toString。
  @override
  String toString() => 'DiomanResponse(${kind.name}, $status, $message)';
}

/// 插件凭空生成失败的输入。
class DiomanFailure {
  /// 指定 [kind] 和细分字段，例如 `DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.offline)`。
  const DiomanFailure(
      {required this.kind,
      this.status = 0,
      this.message = '',
      this.reason,
      this.data,
      this.error,
      this.errorStack});

  /// 类别。
  final DiomanKind kind;

  /// 逻辑状态。
  final int status;

  /// 消息。
  final String message;

  /// 细分原因。
  final String? reason;

  /// 失败附带数据。
  final Object? data;

  /// 原始异常。
  final Object? error;

  /// 原始栈。
  final StackTrace? errorStack;
}

/// 响应判定服务；钩子只在收到适用响应时执行。
class DiomanResponseFactory<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 定制信封与判定，例如 `DiomanResponseFactory(envelope: null)`。
  const DiomanResponseFactory(
      {this.envelope = const Envelope(),
      this.isEnvelope,
      this.isSuccess,
      this.logicalStatus,
      this.expectJson = false,
      this.create});

  /// null 关闭信封识别。
  final Envelope? envelope;

  /// 自定义信封识别，call.envelope=false 优先。
  final bool Function(Map<dynamic, dynamic>)? isEnvelope;

  /// 只对 2xx 信封调用的成功判定。
  final bool Function(Object?)? isSuccess;

  /// 收到响应后的逻辑状态映射。
  final int Function(DiomanResponse<dynamic>)? logicalStatus;

  /// 声明 JSON 但仍是非空字符串时判 parse。
  final bool expectJson;

  /// 构造扩展结果；抛错时内核使用基础结果，避免再次调用坏钩子。
  final R Function(DiomanResponse<dynamic>)? create;

  /// 经扩展构造器创建结果；坏工厂回退 internal 并保留首次程序异常。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> _finish(DiomanResponse<dynamic> init) {
    try {
      return create == null ? init : create!(init);
    } catch (e, s) {
      return _internal(init.request, init.error ?? e, init.errorStack ?? s);
    }
  }

  /// 带调用点派生结果并重新经过扩展构造器；不修改共享结果。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> withCallSite(
          DiomanResponse<dynamic> result, StackTrace callSite) =>
      _finish(result.copy<dynamic>(callSite: callSite));

  /// 直接构造不依赖用户回调的 internal。
  static DiomanResponse<dynamic> _internal(
          DiomanRequest request, Object error, StackTrace stack) =>
      DiomanResponse(
          ok: false,
          kind: DiomanKind.internal,
          status: 0,
          httpStatus: 0,
          data: null,
          request: request,
          error: error,
          errorStack: stack);

  /// 将 [failure] 经过扩展点创建结果；例如 `factory.fail(request, failure)`。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> fail(DiomanRequest request, DiomanFailure failure) =>
      _finish(DiomanResponse(
          ok: failure.kind == DiomanKind.ok,
          kind: failure.kind,
          status: failure.status,
          httpStatus: 0,
          message: failure.message,
          data: failure.data,
          request: request,
          reason: failure.reason,
          error: failure.error,
          errorStack: failure.errorStack));

  /// 分类响应或异常；[cancelled] 的优先级最高，任何钩子异常都归 internal。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> normalize(DiomanRequest request,
      {Response<dynamic>? raw,
      Object? error,
      StackTrace? stack,
      bool cancelled = false}) {
    try {
      if (cancelled) {
        return fail(
            request,
            DiomanFailure(
                kind: DiomanKind.cancel, error: error, errorStack: stack));
      }
      if (error != null) {
        var kind = DiomanKind.internal;
        var status = 0;
        if (error is DioException) {
          switch (error.type) {
            case DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout:
            case DioExceptionType.sendTimeout:
            case DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout:
              kind = DiomanKind.timeout;
            case DioExceptionType.connectionError:
            case DioExceptionType.badCertificate:
              kind = DiomanKind.network;
            case DioExceptionType.badResponse:
              kind = DiomanKind.http;
              status = error.response?.statusCode ?? 0;
            case DioExceptionType.unknown:
              final cause = error.error;
              if (cause is SocketException ||
                  cause is HttpException ||
                  cause is TlsException) {
                kind = DiomanKind.network;
              }
            case DioExceptionType.cancel:
              return fail(
                  request,
                  DiomanFailure(
                      kind: DiomanKind.cancel,
                      error: error,
                      errorStack: stack));
            case DioExceptionType.transformTimeout:
              break;
          }
        }
        final cause = error is DioException ? error.error ?? error : error;
        return _finish(DiomanResponse(
            ok: false,
            kind: kind,
            status: status,
            httpStatus: status,
            data: null,
            request: request,
            error: cause,
            errorStack: stack ?? StackTrace.current,
            raw: error is DioException ? error.response : null));
      }
      final response = raw!;
      final http = response.statusCode ?? 0;
      final httpOk = http >= 200 && http < 300;
      final body = response.data;
      if (httpOk &&
          expectJson &&
          body is String &&
          body.isNotEmpty &&
          (response.headers.value(Headers.contentTypeHeader) ?? '')
              .toLowerCase()
              .contains('json')) {
        return _finish(DiomanResponse(
            ok: false,
            kind: DiomanKind.parse,
            status: http,
            httpStatus: http,
            data: body,
            request: request,
            raw: response));
      }
      final env = envelope;
      final wrapped = request.call.envelope != false &&
          env != null &&
          body is Map &&
          (isEnvelope?.call(body) ??
              (body.containsKey(env.code) &&
                  (body.containsKey(env.data) ||
                      body.containsKey(env.message))));
      final code = wrapped ? (body)[env.code] : null;
      final ok = httpOk &&
          (!wrapped ||
              (isSuccess?.call(code) ??
                  (code == null || code == 0 || code == '0')));
      final status = !ok && httpOk && code is int && code >= 400 && code <= 599
          ? code
          : http;
      var result = DiomanResponse<dynamic>(
          ok: ok,
          kind:
              ok ? DiomanKind.ok : (httpOk ? DiomanKind.biz : DiomanKind.http),
          status: status,
          httpStatus: http,
          code: code,
          message: wrapped ? '${(body)[env.message] ?? ''}' : '',
          data: wrapped ? (body)[env.data] : body,
          request: request,
          raw: response);
      if (logicalStatus != null) {
        result = DiomanResponse(
            ok: result.ok,
            kind: result.kind,
            status: logicalStatus!(result),
            httpStatus: http,
            code: code,
            message: result.message,
            data: result.data,
            request: request,
            raw: response);
      }
      return _finish(result);
    } catch (e, s) {
      return _internal(request, e, s);
    }
  }
}

### stages.dart
/// 洋葱管线从外到内的固定阶段。
enum DiomanStage { outer, prepare, attempt, credential, source }

/// 自定义插件在同阶段内置插件之前或之后运行。
enum DiomanEnforce { pre, post }

/// 内置名称的位置表；位置只由名称决定。
const diomanBuiltinStages = <DiomanStage, List<String>>{
  DiomanStage.outer: ['dioman:loading', 'dioman:notify'],
  DiomanStage.prepare: ['dioman:repath', 'dioman:filter', 'dioman:timeout'],
  DiomanStage.attempt: ['dioman:retry', 'dioman:breaker'],
  DiomanStage.credential: ['dioman:auth'],
  DiomanStage.source: [
    'dioman:mock',
    'dioman:share',
    'dioman:cache',
    'dioman:offline'
  ],
};

### transport.dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'response.dart';

/// 全部 HTTP 状态交给工厂，不让 Dio 构造 badResponse。
bool _acceptStatus(int? _) => true;

/// 递归克隆 JSON 请求体；FormData 使用 Dio 的 clone，其他类型保留身份。
Object? _clone(Object? value, DiomanLogger log) {
  if (value is Map) return value.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, _clone(v, log)));
  if (value is List) return value.map((v) => _clone(v, log)).toList();
  if (value is FormData) return value.clone();
  if (value != null && value is! num && value is! bool && value is! String) {
    log('请求体无法克隆，回退原引用');
  }
  return value;
}

/// 执行一次发送；传输构造、Dio 及工厂异常都在本边界归一化。
Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    diomanTransport<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
        Dio dio, DiomanContext<R> ctx) async {
  try {
    final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
    if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
    final options = ctx.request.toRequestOptions()
      ..validateStatus = _acceptStatus
      ..cancelToken = ctx.cancelToken;
    if (ctx.replayable) options.data = _clone(options.data, ctx.log);
    final raw = await dio.fetch<dynamic>(options);
    return ctx.factory
        .normalize(ctx.request, raw: raw, cancelled: ctx.isCancelled);
  } catch (e, s) {
    return ctx.factory
        .normalize(ctx.request, error: e, stack: s, cancelled: ctx.isCancelled);
  }
}

import 'context.dart';
import 'plugin.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';

/// 编译后的上下文执行器。
typedef DiomanRunner<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    = Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> Function(DiomanContext<R> ctx);

/// 已安装的配置及实例。
class DiomanRegistration<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 保存配置与客户端实例。
  const DiomanRegistration(this.plugin, this.instance);

  /// 插件配置。
  final DiomanPlugin<R> plugin;

  /// setup 返回的独立状态。
  final DiomanPluginInstance<R>? instance;
}

/// 按名称确定位置；自定义插件位于同阶段内置插件前后。
(int, int) _position(DiomanPlugin plugin) {
  for (final entry in diomanBuiltinStages.entries) {
    final index = entry.value.indexOf(plugin.name);
    if (index >= 0) return (entry.key.index, index);
  }
  if (plugin.name.startsWith('dioman:')) {
    throw ArgumentError.value(plugin.name, 'name', '保留前缀');
  }
  return (plugin.stage.index, plugin.enforce == DiomanEnforce.pre ? -1 : 100);
}

/// 在 setup 前验证重名和保留前缀，拒绝非法配置且不产生副作用。
void validateDiomanPlugins(Iterable<DiomanPlugin> plugins) {
  final names = <String>{};
  for (final plugin in plugins) {
    if (!names.add(plugin.name)) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(plugin.name, 'name', '插件重名');
    }
    _position(plugin);
  }
}

/// 稳定排序已启用且有 handle 的插件；不修改输入。
List<DiomanRegistration<R>>
    sortDiomanPipeline<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
        List<DiomanRegistration<R>> registrations) {
  final indexed =
      registrations.indexed.where((e) => e.$2.instance != null).toList();
  indexed.sort((a, b) {
    final x = _position(a.$2.plugin);
    final y = _position(b.$2.plugin);
    final stage = x.$1.compareTo(y.$1);
    if (stage != 0) return stage;
    final order = x.$2.compareTo(y.$2);
    return order == 0 ? a.$1.compareTo(b.$1) : order;
  });
  return indexed.map((e) => e.$2).toList();
}

/// 从内到外编译一次；每层含一个异常边界，无补丁复用上下文。
DiomanRunner<R> compileDiomanPipeline<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
    List<DiomanRegistration<R>> layers, DiomanRunner<R> transport) {
  var run = transport;
  for (final layer in layers.reversed) {
    final inner = run;
    run = (ctx) async {
      try {
        return await layer.instance!.handle(ctx, ([patch]) async {
          DiomanContext<R> target;
          try {
            target = patch == null ? ctx : ctx.derive(patch);
          } catch (e, s) {
            return ctx.fail(DiomanFailure(
                kind: DiomanKind.internal, error: e, errorStack: s));
          }
          return inner(target);
        });
      } catch (e, s) {
        return ctx.fail(
            DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.internal, error: e, errorStack: s));
      }
    };
  }
  return run;
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 客户端和请求级的构键配置；空字段继承客户端值。
class DiomanKeyOptions {
  /// 创建配置，例如 `DiomanKeyOptions(fastMode: true)`。
  const DiomanKeyOptions(
      {this.fastMode, this.ignores, this.builder, this.namespace, this.key});

  /// 快速模式只编码方法和路径。
  final bool? fastMode;

  /// 与客户端取并集的忽略字段。
  final List<String>? ignores;

  /// 自定义构键器；抛错按程序异常处理。
  final String Function(RequestOptions)? builder;

  /// 客户端账号作用域，不应返回敏感令牌。
  final String? Function()? namespace;

  /// 请求级显式键；空串沿用自动计算。
  final String? key;
}

/// 与旧版算法独立共存的构键服务。
class DiomanKeyService {
  /// 创建服务，例如 `DiomanKeyService(const DiomanKeyOptions())`。
  const DiomanKeyService(this.options);

  /// 客户端构键配置。
  final DiomanKeyOptions options;

  /// 按请求 builder、请求 key、客户端 builder、计算的次序返回键；false 返回 null。
  String? build(RequestOptions request, [Object? override]) {
    if (override == false) return null;
    if (override != null &&
        override is! String &&
        override is! DiomanKeyOptions) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(override, 'key');
    }
    final o = override is DiomanKeyOptions ? override : null;
    final scope = options.namespace?.call();
    final explicit = override is String ? override : o?.key;
    if (o?.builder != null) return _scopedKey(o!.builder!(request), scope);
    if (explicit != null && explicit.isNotEmpty) {
      return _scopedKey(explicit, scope);
    }
    if (options.builder != null) {
      return _scopedKey(options.builder!(request), scope);
    }
    final ignores = _merge(options.ignores ?? const [], o?.ignores);
    return (o?.fastMode ?? options.fastMode ?? false)
        ? _scopedKey(_fastKey(request), scope)
        : _deepKey(request, ignores, scope);
  }

  /// 为快速键或自定义键增加独立作用域字段，空作用域保持原键。
  static String _scopedKey(String key, String? scope) =>
      scope == null || scope.isEmpty ? key : jsonEncode([key, scope]);

  /// Merges [override] into [base] (union), or returns [base] unchanged when
  /// [override] is `null`.
  ///
  /// 把[override]与[base]取并集合并；[override]为`null`时原样返回[base]。
  static List<String> _merge(List<String> base, List<String>? override) =>
      override == null ? base : {...base, ...override}.toList();

  /// 使用方法、来源和路径生成快速键。
  String _fastKey(RequestOptions o) =>
      '${o.method.toUpperCase()}:${o.uri.hasAuthority ? o.uri.origin : ''}${o.uri.path}';

  /// 分字段编码规范化查询及请求体，保留非结构化对象的身份。
  String _deepKey(RequestOptions o, List<String> ignoreKeys, String? scope) {
    final uri = o.uri;
    final params = <String, dynamic>{
      for (final e in uri.queryParametersAll.entries)
        if (!ignoreKeys.contains(e.key))
          e.key: e.value.length == 1 ? e.value.single : e.value,
    };
    final data = o.data;
    Object? body;
    if (data is Map) {
      final filtered = <String, dynamic>{
        for (final e in data.entries)
          if (!ignoreKeys.contains('${e.key}')) '${e.key}': e.value,
      };
      if (filtered.isNotEmpty) body = {'map': _encode(filtered)};
    } else if (data is String && data.isNotEmpty) {
      body = {'string': data};
    } else if (data != null) {
      body = {'identity': identityHashCode(data)};
    }
    return jsonEncode([
      _fastKey(o),
      _encode(params),
      body,
      if (scope != null && scope.isNotEmpty) scope,
    ]);
  }

  /// 递归排序Map字段并保留List顺序后编码，无法序列化时保留原Map的字符串表示。
  static String _encode(Map v) {
    try {
      return jsonEncode(_normalize(v));
    } catch (_) {
      return v.toString();
    }
  }

  /// 返回递归规范化的副本：Map键按字符串比较排序，List保序，其他值原样保留。
  static Object? _normalize(Object? v) {
    if (v is Map) {
      final entries = v.entries.toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => '${a.key}'.compareTo('${b.key}'));
      return Map.fromEntries(
        entries.map((e) => MapEntry(e.key, _normalize(e.value))),
      );
    }
    if (v is List) return v.map(_normalize).toList();
    return v;
  }
}
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 插件请求级选项键；按对象身份隔离，建议使用静态 final 实例。
class DiomanOptionKey<T> {
  /// [description] 仅供调试，例如 `DiomanOptionKey<int>('retry')`。
  DiomanOptionKey(this.description);

  /// 调试说明。
  final String description;
}

/// 解析统一选项；省略/true 复用 [base]，false 关闭；[optIn] 控制省略时关闭。
/// [merge] 合并类型化覆盖，[shorthand] 返回简写解析值；未知类型抛 ArgumentError。
/// 示例：`resolveOption<int>(true, base: 3, merge: (a, b) => b)`。
T? resolveOption<T>(Object? value,
    {required T base,
    required T Function(T base, T override) merge,
    T? Function(Object value)? shorthand,
    bool optIn = false}) {
  if (value == false || (value == null && optIn)) return null;
  if (value == null || value == true) return base;
  if (value is T) return merge(base, value as T);
  final short = shorthand?.call(value);
  if (short != null) return merge(base, short);
  throw ArgumentError.value(value, 'value', '不支持的插件选项');
}

/// 一次逻辑调用的配置；省略字段沿用 Dio 或客户端配置。
class DiomanCall {
  /// 创建调用配置，例如 `DiomanCall(key: false, envelope: false)`。
  const DiomanCall(
      {this.headers,
      this.connectTimeout,
      this.sendTimeout,
      this.receiveTimeout,
      this.cancelToken,
      this.key,
      this.envelope,
      this.silent = false,
      this.options = const {}});

  /// 按不区分大小写的键覆盖请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic>? headers;

  /// 连接超时。
  final Duration? connectTimeout;

  /// 发送超时。
  final Duration? sendTimeout;

  /// 接收超时。
  final Duration? receiveTimeout;

  /// 用户取消令牌。
  final CancelToken? cancelToken;

  /// String、false 或 DiomanKeyOptions；null 为自动计算。
  final Object? key;

  /// false 禁用本次信封识别。
  final bool? envelope;

  /// 是否静默通知。
  final bool silent;

  /// 插件的类型化请求级选项。
  final Map<DiomanOptionKey, Object?> options;
}

  /// 写入 [key] 的类型化 [value]，各层共享。
  void set<T>(StateKey<T> key, T value) => (state.values ??= {})[key] = value;

  /// 经工厂生成结果；坏扩展工厂回退基础 internal，以保证 next 不抛错。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> fail(DiomanFailure failure) =>
      factory.fail(request, failure);

  /// 声明可能重放；后续每次发送均独立克隆请求体。
  void markReplayable() => state.replayable = true;

  /// 已取消则造 cancel 结果，否则返回 null；每次 await 后应调用。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic>? cancelled() =>
      isCancelled ? fail(const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.cancel)) : null;

  /// 派生请求视图，保留所有逻辑服务与共享状态。
  DiomanContext<R> derive(RequestPatch patch) => DiomanContext(
      id: id,
      client: client,
      request: request.patch(patch),
      state: state,
      factory: factory,
      keyService: keyService,
      clock: clock,
      log: log);

  /// 等待 [pending]、取消或 [timeout]；后两者返回 null，结束后无订阅及计时器。
  /// 已取消立即返回；迟到的值或异常被消费且不会改变结局。
  Future<T?> wait<T>(Future<T> pending, {Duration? timeout}) {
    final result = Completer<T?>();
    Timer? timer;
    DiomanCancelLink? localLink;
    late void Function() cancel;
    void clean() {
      timer?.cancel();
      localLink?.dispose();
      state.listeners.remove(cancel);
    }

    void finish(T? value) {
      if (result.isCompleted) return;
      clean();
      result.complete(value);
    }

    cancel = () => finish(null);
    // 即使已经取消，也消费输入的迟到异常。
    pending.then((value) => finish(value),
        onError: (Object error, StackTrace stack) {
      if (result.isCompleted) return;
      clean();
      result.completeError(error, stack);
    });
    if (isCancelled) {
      finish(null);
      return result.future;
    }
    state.listeners[cancel] = cancelToken;
    if (!identical(cancelToken, state.token)) {
      localLink = DiomanCancelLink(cancelToken, (_) => cancel());
    }
    if (timeout != null) timer = clock.createTimer(timeout, cancel);
    return result.future;
  }
}

"""从原始 LCOV 检查六个内核文件，拒绝缺失文件和零计数。"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FILES = ('client', 'pipeline', 'context', 'request', 'response', 'transport')


def inspect(path):
    """输出逐文件 LF/LH/BRF/BRH，并对原始命中记录独立复算。"""
    found = {}
    for block in path.read_text(encoding='utf-8').split('end_of_record'):
        lines = block.splitlines()
        source = next((line[3:].replace('\\', '/') for line in lines if line.startswith('SF:')), '')
        if '/lib/src/pipeline/' not in source:
            continue
        name = source.rsplit('/', 1)[-1].removesuffix('.dart')
        if name not in FILES:
            continue
        assert name not in found, f'重复文件 {source}'
        rows = [line[3:].split(',') for line in lines if line.startswith('DA:')]
        branches = [line[5:].split(',') for line in lines if line.startswith('BRDA:')]
        counts = (len(rows), sum(int(row[1]) > 0 for row in rows),
                  len(branches), sum(row[3] != '-' and int(row[3]) > 0 for row in branches))
        declared = {line.split(':')[0]: int(line.split(':')[1]) for line in lines
                    if line.startswith(('LF:', 'LH:', 'BRF:', 'BRH:'))}
        assert counts == tuple(declared[k] for k in ('LF', 'LH', 'BRF', 'BRH'))
        found[name] = counts
    assert set(found) == set(FILES), f'缺文件: {set(FILES) - set(found)}'
    print('file LF LH BRF BRH')
    for name in FILES:
        lf, lh, brf, brh = found[name]
        print(name + '.dart', lf, lh, brf, brh)
        assert lf > 0 and brf > 0 and lf == lh and brf == brh, name
    print('six files present; nonzero counters; lines=100%; branches=100%; PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    inspect(ROOT / (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'coverage/b1.info'))
"""B1 定向变异：每次只改新内核文件，失败后 finally 恢复并核对 SHA256。"""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST = ROOT / 'docs/reports/pipeline-b1-evidence'
CASES = [
    ('wait-cleanup', 'context', 'timer?.cancel();', '/* mutation: timer not cancelled */',
     'test/unit/context_test.dart', '正常值、异常、超时'),
    ('already-cancelled', 'context', 'if (isCancelled) {', 'if (false) {',
     'test/unit/context_test.dart', '已取消立即返回'),
    ('layer-boundary', 'pipeline', 'kind: DiomanKind.internal', 'kind: DiomanKind.offline',
     'test/unit/pipeline_test.dart', '中间件异常转 internal'),
    ('settlement', 'client', 'if (!result.ok) throw', 'if (false) throw',
     'test/rule/client_rule_test.dart', '九格落定'),
    ('patch-headers', 'request', 'h.removeWhere((k, _) => k.toLowerCase() == key.toLowerCase());', '/* mutation: header deletion omitted */',
     'test/unit/request_options_test.dart', '合并、大小写删除'),
    ('factory-classification', 'response', 'code == 0', 'code == 1',
     'test/unit/response_test.dart', '终审识别'),
    ('pending-unregister', 'client', '_pending.remove(state);', '/* mutation: pending leaked */',
     'test/boundary/client_boundary_test.dart', '登记后 compose'),
    ('replay-clone-I7', 'transport', 'options.data = _clone(options.data, ctx.log);', 'options.data = options.data;',
     'test/rule/transport_rule_test.dart', 'I7 嵌套请求体'),
]


def run():
    """逐项执行，不允许编译失败冒充杀死变异，保存失败标题及恢复哈希。"""
    DEST.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dart = shutil.which('dart')
    results = []
    for name, module, old, new, test, title in CASES:
        path = ROOT / f'lib/src/pipeline/{module}.dart'
        original = path.read_bytes()
        digest = hashlib.sha256(original).hexdigest()
        text = original.decode('utf-8')
        assert old in text, (name, old)
        try:
            path.write_text(text.replace(old, new), encoding='utf-8')
            command = [dart, 'test', test, '--name', title, '--reporter', 'expanded', '--timeout', '5s']
            proc = subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                  encoding='utf-8', errors='replace', timeout=60)
            output = proc.stdout + proc.stderr
            (DEST / (name + '.txt')).write_text(output, encoding='utf-8')
            assert proc.returncode != 0 and '[E]' in output and 'Some tests failed' in output, name
            assert 'Failed to load' not in output and 'Compilation failed' not in output, name
        finally:
            path.write_bytes(original)
        assert hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest
        results.append({'mutation': name, 'test': test, 'title': title, 'killed': True,
                        'restored_sha256': digest})
        print(name + ': test failed; source restored; PASS', flush=True)
    (DEST / 'mutations.json').write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    run()

              fail('必须抛出 bug');

              expect(s.toString(), contains('original-stack'));
            }
          }
          expect(rejected, 0);

          expect(r.kind, kind);

            expect(rejected, 1);
          }

        expect(api.pending, 0);
