  test('[Dioman.pending] 登记后 compose/上下文构造抛错仍注销并解除用户订阅', () async {
    final api = testClient();
    final token = CancelToken();
    await expectLater(
        api.send<Object?>('GET', '/', call: _BadCall(token)), throwsStateError);
    expect(api.pending, 0);
    expect(api.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    token.cancel();
    await flush();
    expect(await api.get<String>('/next'), 'ok');
  });

  test('[Dioman.use] 重名和保留前缀先拒绝 setup，安装失败原链不变', () async {
    final api = testClient(plugins: [TestPlugin('a')]);
    final duplicate = TestPlugin('a');
    expect(() => api.use(duplicate), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(duplicate.setups, 0);
    final reserved = TestPlugin('dioman:unknown');
    expect(() => api.use(reserved), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(reserved.setups, 0);
    final bad = TestPlugin('bad', onSetup: () => throw StateError('setup'));
    expect(() => api.use(bad), throwsStateError);
    expect(api.inspect().map((e) => e.$2), ['a']);
    final disabled = TestPlugin('disabled', enabled: false);
    api.use(disabled);
    expect(disabled.setups, 0);
    api.use(TestPlugin('setup-only', setupOnly: true));
    expect(api.inspect().length, 1);
    expect(api.eject('absent'), isFalse);
    expect(api.eject('disabled'), isTrue);
    expect(api.eject('a'), isTrue);
    expect(api.plugin<TestInstance>(), isNull);
    expect(await api.get<String>('/'), 'ok');
  });

  test('[Dioman.inspect] 内置按名定位和在途保留链快照', () async {
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    var calls = 0;
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      TestPlugin('gate', stage: DiomanStage.outer, enforce: DiomanEnforce.pre,
          handler: (ctx, next) async {
        await gate.future;
        return next();
      }),
      TestPlugin('dioman:cache', stage: DiomanStage.outer, handler: (_, next) {
        calls++;
        return next();
      }),
    ]);
    expect(api.inspect().last, (DiomanStage.source, 'dioman:cache'));
    final pending = api.send<Object?>('GET', '/');
    expect(api.eject('dioman:cache'), isTrue);
    api.use(TestPlugin('new'));
    gate.complete();
    await pending;
    expect(calls, 1);
    await api.get<Object?>('/');
    expect(calls, 1);
    expect(() => api.inspect().clear(), throwsUnsupportedError);
  });

  test('[Dioman.extend] 重新 setup，配置深拷贝和实例状态隔离', () async {
    final p = TestPlugin('state');
    final api = testClient(plugins: [p]);
    api.dio.options.extra['nested'] = {
      'list': [1]
    };
    final child = api.extend();
    child.dio.httpClientAdapter = api.dio.httpClientAdapter;
    expect(p.setups, 2);
    await api.get<Object?>('/');
    expect(api.plugin<TestInstance>()!.count, 1);
    expect(child.plugin<TestInstance>()!.count, 0);
    ((child.dio.options.extra['nested'] as Map)['list'] as List).add(2);
    expect((api.dio.options.extra['nested'] as Map)['list'], [1]);
    final overridden = api.extend(
        options: BaseOptions(baseUrl: 'https://other.test'), plugins: []);
    expect(overridden.dio.options.baseUrl, 'https://other.test');
    expect(overridden.inspect(), isEmpty);
    child.dispose();
    api.dispose();
    overridden.dispose();
  });

  test('[Dioman.dispose] 先取消，再逆序尽力释放，抛首错且拒绝后续请求', () async {
    final order = <String>[];
    final first = StateError('first');
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      TestPlugin('a', onDispose: () {
        order.add('a');
        throw StateError('last');
      }),
      TestPlugin('b', onDispose: () {
        order.add('b');
        throw first;
      }),
      TestPlugin('wait',
          handler: (ctx, _) async {
            await ctx.wait(Completer<void>().future);
            return ctx.cancelled()!;
          },
          onDispose: () => order.add('wait')),
    ]);
    final pending = api.send<Object?>('GET', '/');
    expect(() => api.dispose(), throwsA(same(first)));
    expect(order, ['wait', 'b', 'a']);
    expect((await pending).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    api.dispose();
    await expectLater(api.send<Object?>('GET', '/'), throwsStateError);
    expect(() => api.use(TestPlugin('new')), throwsStateError);
    expect(() => api.extend(), throwsStateError);
    expect(api.pending, 0);
  });

  test('[Dioman.new] 无效 debug、构造前重名验证和失败 setup 释放已安装实例', () {
    expect(() => testClient(debug: 3), throwsArgumentError);
    final p = TestPlugin('a');
    expect(() => testClient(plugins: [p, p]), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(p.setups, 0);
    final events = <String>[];
    expect(
        () => testClient(plugins: [
              TestPlugin('disabled', enabled: false),
              TestPlugin('ok', onDispose: () {
                events.add('dispose');
                throw StateError('cleanup');
              }),
              TestPlugin('setup-only', setupOnly: true),
              TestPlugin('bad', onSetup: () => throw ArgumentError('original'))
            ]),
        throwsArgumentError);
    expect(events, ['dispose']);
    testClient().dispose();
  });
}

/// 在请求登记后模拟配置 getter 抛错。
class _BadCall extends DiomanCall {
  /// 先提供可联动用户令牌。
  _BadCall(CancelToken token) : super(cancelToken: token);

  /// 在 compose 前抛错。
  @override
  Map<String, dynamic>? get headers => throw StateError('construct');
}
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

/// 工厂规则表和扩展点的纯单元验证。
void main() {
  final request = DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(path: '/r'));
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> normalize(Object? body,
          {int? status = 200,
          DiomanResponseFactory factory = const DiomanResponseFactory(),
          DiomanRequest? req,
          bool json = false}) =>
      factory.normalize(req ?? request,
          raw: Response<dynamic>(
              requestOptions: request.toRequestOptions(),
              data: body,
              statusCode: status,
              headers: Headers.fromMap({
                if (json) Headers.contentTypeHeader: [Headers.jsonContentType]
              })));

  test('[DiomanResponseFactory.normalize] 分类规则逐行：取消、超时、网络、HTTP、bug', () {
    const factory = DiomanResponseFactory();
    final table = <(DioExceptionType, Object?, DiomanKind)>[
      (DioExceptionType.cancel, null, DiomanKind.cancel),
      for (final type in [
        DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout,
        DioExceptionType.sendTimeout,
        DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout
      ])
        (type, null, DiomanKind.timeout),
      (DioExceptionType.connectionError, null, DiomanKind.network),
      (DioExceptionType.badCertificate, null, DiomanKind.network),
      (
        DioExceptionType.unknown,
        const SocketException('socket'),
        DiomanKind.network
      ),
      (
        DioExceptionType.unknown,
        const HttpException('http'),
        DiomanKind.network
      ),
      (DioExceptionType.unknown, const TlsException('tls'), DiomanKind.network),
      (DioExceptionType.unknown, StateError('bug'), DiomanKind.internal),
      (DioExceptionType.unknown, null, DiomanKind.internal),
      (DioExceptionType.badResponse, null, DiomanKind.http),
      (DioExceptionType.transformTimeout, null, DiomanKind.internal),
    ];
    for (final row in table) {
      final error = DioException(
          requestOptions: request.toRequestOptions(),
          type: row.$1,
          error: row.$2,
          response: Response(
              requestOptions: request.toRequestOptions(), statusCode: 503));
      final r = factory.normalize(request, error: error);
      expect(r.kind, row.$3);
      expect(r.status, row.$1 == DioExceptionType.badResponse ? 503 : 0);
      expect(
          r.error,
          row.$1 == DioExceptionType.cancel
              ? same(error)
              : same(row.$2 ?? error));
    }
    final noResponse = DioException(
        requestOptions: request.toRequestOptions(),
        type: DioExceptionType.badResponse);
    expect(factory.normalize(request, error: noResponse).status, 0);
    expect(factory.normalize(request, cancelled: true).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    final bug = StateError('native');
    final stack = StackTrace.fromString('original');
    final r = factory.normalize(request, error: bug, stack: stack);
    expect(r.error, same(bug));
    expect(r.errorStack, same(stack));
  });

  test('[DiomanResponseFactory.new(envelope)] 终审识别、开关、业务码边界及逻辑状态', () {
    expect(normalize({'code': 0, 'data': 7}).data, 7);
    expect(normalize({'code': 0, 'data': 7}).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
    expect(normalize({'code': '0', 'message': 'ok'}).ok, isTrue);
    expect(normalize({'code': null, 'data': null}).ok, isTrue);
    expect(normalize({'code': 401, 'message': 'expired'}).status, 401);
    expect(normalize({'code': 'TOKEN', 'data': null}).kind, DiomanKind.biz);
    expect(normalize({'code': 399, 'data': null}).status, 200);
    expect(normalize({'code': 600, 'data': null}).status, 200);
    expect(normalize({'code': 599, 'data': null}).status, 599);
    expect(normalize({'code': 400, 'data': null}).status, 400);
    for (final body in [
      null,
      '',
      'raw',
      {'code': 401},
      {'data': 1},
      {'message': 'x'}
    ]) {
      expect(normalize(body).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
    }
    expect(normalize('x', status: null).kind, DiomanKind.http);
    expect(normalize('x', status: 199).kind, DiomanKind.http);
    expect(normalize('x', status: 300).kind, DiomanKind.http);
    expect(
        normalize({'code': 0, 'data': 1}, status: 500).kind, DiomanKind.http);
    final plain = DiomanRequest(request.toRequestOptions(),
        call: const DiomanCall(envelope: false));
    expect(normalize({'code': 500, 'data': 1}, req: plain).ok, isTrue);
    expect(
        normalize({'code': 500, 'data': 1},
                factory: const DiomanResponseFactory(envelope: null))
            .ok,
        isTrue);
    final custom = DiomanResponseFactory(
        envelope: const Envelope(code: 's', data: 'd', message: 'm'),
        isEnvelope: (map) => map.containsKey('s'),
        isSuccess: (code) => code == 'yes',
        logicalStatus: (r) => r.ok ? 201 : 403);
    expect(normalize({'s': 'yes', 'd': 8}, factory: custom).status, 201);
    expect(normalize({'s': 'no'}, factory: custom).status, 403);
    expect(normalize({'d': 1}, factory: custom).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
  });

  test('[DiomanResponseFactory.new(expectJson)] 只对 2xx 非空声明 JSON 字符串判 parse',
      () {
    const factory = DiomanResponseFactory(expectJson: true);
    expect(normalize('broken', factory: factory, json: true).kind,
        DiomanKind.parse);
    expect(normalize('', factory: factory, json: true).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
    expect(normalize('broken', factory: factory).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
    expect(normalize({}, factory: factory, json: true).kind, DiomanKind.ok);
    expect(normalize('broken', factory: factory, json: true, status: 500).kind,
        DiomanKind.http);
  });

  test('[DiomanResponseFactory.create] 钩子 bug 和坏工厂回退保留首次异常', () {
    final bug = StateError('first');
    final second = StateError('second');
    final stack = StackTrace.fromString('first-stack');
    for (final factory in [
      DiomanResponseFactory(isEnvelope: (_) => throw bug),
      DiomanResponseFactory(isSuccess: (_) => throw bug),
      DiomanResponseFactory(logicalStatus: (_) => throw bug),
      DiomanResponseFactory(create: (_) => throw bug),
    ]) {
      final r = normalize({'code': 0, 'data': null}, factory: factory);
      expect(r.kind, DiomanKind.internal);
      expect(r.error, same(bug));
      expect(r.errorStack, isNotNull);
    }
    final factory = DiomanResponseFactory(create: (_) => throw second);
    final r = factory.fail(
        request,
        DiomanFailure(
            kind: DiomanKind.internal, error: bug, errorStack: stack));
    expect(r.error, same(bug));
    expect(r.errorStack, same(stack));
    expect(
        factory
            .fail(request, const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.offline))
            .error,
        same(second));
    expect(
        const DiomanResponseFactory()
            .fail(request, const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.ok))
            .ok,
        isTrue);
    final expanded =
        DiomanResponseFactory<ExtraResult>(create: ExtraResult.new);
    expect(normalize(1, factory: expanded), isA<ExtraResult>());
  });

  test('[DiomanResponse.toString] 循环对象与恶意 toString 不影响描述和派生', () {
    final cycle = <Object?>[];
    cycle.add(cycle);
    final r = DiomanResponse(
        ok: false,
        kind: DiomanKind.biz,
        status: 400,
        httpStatus: 200,
        data: cycle,
        request: request,
        error: _BadString(),
        reason: 'denied',
        code: 400);
    expect(r.toString(), contains('biz'));
    final site = StackTrace.current;
    final copy = r.copy<Object?>(callSite: site);
    expect(copy.callSite, same(site));
    expect(copy.copy<Object?>().callSite, same(site));
    expect(r.callSite, isNull);
    expect(copy.data, same(cycle));
  });
}

/// 模拟不能被序列化或描述的用户异常。
class _BadString {
  /// 不允许描述原始异常。
  @override
  String toString() => throw StateError('不得调用');
}

/// 验证结果扩展点。
class ExtraResult extends DiomanResponse<dynamic> {
  /// 从工厂初值创建带扩展字段的结果。
  ExtraResult(DiomanResponse<dynamic> r)
      : super(
            ok: r.ok,
            kind: r.kind,
            status: r.status,
            httpStatus: r.httpStatus,
            data: r.data,
            request: r.request);
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_clock.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';

/// 取消等待的各个终局及逻辑状态共享。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanContext.wait] 替换传输令牌隔离外层取消，逻辑状态仍共享', () async {
    final root = testContext();
    final token = CancelToken();
    final inner = root.derive(RequestPatch(cancelToken: token));
    final gate = Completer<int>();
    final waiting = inner.wait(gate.future);
    root.state.cancel();
    await flush();
    expect(root.isCancelled, isTrue);
    expect(inner.isCancelled, isFalse);
    expect(inner.cancellationSubscriptions, 1);
    gate.complete(7);
    expect(await waiting, 7);
    expect(inner.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    token.cancel();
    await flush();
    expect(inner.isCancelled, isTrue);
  });
  test('[DiomanContext.wait] 正常值、异常、超时、取消均清理且忽略迟到结果', () async {
    for (final outcome in ['value', 'error', 'timeout', 'cancel']) {
      final clock = FakeClock();
      final ctx = testContext(clock: clock);
      final input = Completer<int>();
      final result =
          ctx.wait(input.future, timeout: const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(ctx.cancellationSubscriptions, 1);
      expect(clock.pendingTimers, 1);
      switch (outcome) {
        case 'value':
          input.complete(7);
          expect(await result, 7);
        case 'error':
          input.completeError(StateError('wait'));
          await expectLater(result, throwsStateError);
        case 'timeout':
          clock.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
          expect(await result, isNull);
          expect(ctx.cancelled(), isNull);
        case 'cancel':
          ctx.state.cancel();
          expect(await result, isNull);
          expect(ctx.cancelled()!.kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
      }
      expect(ctx.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
      expect(clock.pendingTimers, 0);
      if (!input.isCompleted) input.completeError(StateError('late'));
      clock.elapse(const Duration(days: 1));
      await flush();
      ctx.state.dispose();
    }
  });

  test('[DiomanContext.wait] 已取消立即返回且不登记，无超时、多等待和替换令牌', () async {
    final token = CancelToken()..cancel();
    final clock = FakeClock();
    final ctx = testContext(token: token, clock: clock);
    expect(await ctx.wait(Future.value(1), timeout: Duration.zero), isNull);
    expect(ctx.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    expect(clock.pendingTimers, 0);
    final late = Completer<int>();
    expect(await ctx.wait(late.future), isNull);
    late.completeError(StateError('late'));
    final active = testContext();
    expect(await active.wait(Future.value(2)), 2);
    final one = active.wait(Completer<int>().future);
    final two = active.wait(Completer<int>().future);
    active.state.token.cancel();
    expect(await one, isNull);
    expect(await two, isNull);
    final custom = CancelToken();
    final derived = testContext().derive(RequestPatch(cancelToken: custom));
    final pending = derived.wait(Completer<int>().future);
    custom.cancel();
    expect(await pending, isNull);
    expect(derived.isCancelled, isTrue);
    expect(derived.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    final done = testContext();
    final finishing = done.wait(Completer<int>().future);
    done.state.dispose();
    expect(await finishing, isNull);
    await flush();
  });

  test('[DiomanContext.key] 每请求缓存、派生重算、类型状态共享及按身份隔离', () {
    var calls = 0;
    final ctx = testContext(key: DiomanKeyOptions(builder: (o) {
      calls++;
      return o.path;
    }));
    final key = StateKey<int>('same');
    final other = StateKey<int>('same');
    expect(ctx.get(key), isNull);
    ctx.set(key, 4);
    expect(ctx.key(), '/unit');
    expect(ctx.key(), '/unit');
    expect(calls, 1);
    final inner = ctx.derive(const RequestPatch(path: '/inner'));
    expect(inner.id, ctx.id);
    expect(inner.client, same(ctx.client));
    expect(inner.get(key), 4);
    expect(inner.get(other), isNull);
    expect(key.description, 'same');
    inner.set(key, 5);
    expect(ctx.get(key), 5);
    expect(inner.key(), '/inner');
    expect(calls, 2);
    expect(ctx.replayable, isFalse);
    inner.markReplayable();
    expect(ctx.replayable, isTrue);
    final noKey = ctx.derive(const RequestPatch());
    expect(noKey.key(), '/unit');
  });
}

"C:\\Users\\jelon\\AppData\\Local\\Microsoft\\WindowsApps\\Microsoft.PowerShell_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\pwsh.exe" -Command "Get-Content docs/design/pipeline-architecture.md | Select-String -Pattern '异常|栈|parse|internal|send<|R |cancel|不可变' -Context 2,3; Get-Content lib/src/response.dart -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 10; Get-Content test/rule/transport_rule_test.dart; Get-Content test/unit/key_parity_test.dart" in E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman
 succeeded in 1317ms:

  
  - `Dioman(dio: …, plugins: […])` 产出客户端。一次调用就是一个逻辑请求，穿过 5 个阶段的中间件，最内层调用一次 `dio.fet
ch`。
> - 传输边界把 Dio 的响应或异常判定成 `DiomanResponse`；链路里只流转它，`next()` 不抛错。最外层落定：`get` 成功返回数据
、失败抛出；`send` 返回结果对象；程序 bug 原样抛出原始异常。
  - 插件 = `DiomanPlugin`（名字、阶段、`setup` 或 `handle`）。内置插件位置固定，自定义插件只选阶段。
> - 请求不可变，内层改请求用 `next(patch)`；重试、重放就是再调一次 `next()`。
  - 决策依据见 [0001](../decisions/0001-pipeline-redesign.md)，插件细则见 [插件规格](pipeline-plugin-spec.md)。
  
  ## 1. 结构
    │
    ▼
> ┌─────────────── 落定（内核）：R → data | R | throw R；bug → 原样抛出 ───────────────┐
> │ outer       loading → notify                        每个逻辑请求一次           │
> │ prepare     repath → filter → timeout               请求整形，一次             │
> │ attempt     retry → breaker                         重试边界：往里每次尝试重跑   │
  │ credential  auth                                    每次尝试注入 token          │
  │ source      mock → share → cache → offline          结果来源，可短路            │
> │ ┌──────── 传输（内核）：dio.fetch（使用者的 Dio 拦截器在这里运行）+ 判定 → R ────┐│
  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  ```
  
  | 文件 | 职责 |
  |---|---|
> | `client.dart` | `Dioman`：动词方法、`request/send`、建逻辑请求上下文、登记在途、落定、`use/eject/inspect/cancelAll/
pending/extend/dispose` |
> | `pipeline.dart` | 插件排序（阶段 + 内置位置表）、链编译、`next(patch)`、每层的异常边界 |
  | `stages.dart` | `DiomanStage` 枚举与内置插件位置表 |
> | `context.dart` | `DiomanContext`：请求视图、取消、状态键、`key()`、`fail()`、`markReplayable()`、`wait()`、`cancell
ed()` |
> | `request.dart` | 不可变的 `DiomanRequest`、`RequestPatch`、与 `RequestOptions` 互转 |
  | `transport.dart` | 按需克隆请求体、调用 `dio.fetch`（`validateStatus` 全部放行）、交给工厂判定 |
  | `response.dart` | `DiomanKind`、`DiomanResponse`、`DiomanResponseFactory`、`Envelope` |
  | `options.dart` | 请求级插件选项的统一解析（`resolveOption`） |
  | `token_manager.dart` | `DiomanTokenManager` 接口（去掉 `canRefresh`，见插件规格 auth） |
  
> 删除：`dioman.dart` 里的 `install/installPlugins/DiomanHandle`、`plugin_order.dart`、`replay_support.dart`、`request_
claims.dart`、`extra_keys.dart`、`mock_adapter.dart`，以及 `key/cancel/normalize/log` 四个插件。
  
  ## 2. 一次请求的数据流
  
> 1. 动词方法把 path、query、data、调用级配置交给内核。内核用 `Options.compose(dio.options, path, …)` **合并一次**，得
到有效的 `RequestOptions`，再包成不可变的 `DiomanRequest`（保留有效配置的全部字段）。`dio.fetch` 不会再合并 BaseOptions
（dio 5.11.1 已核对）。
> 2. 内核建逻辑请求级上下文：分配 `id`；建内核 `CancelToken`，并与使用者传入的 token 联动；登记到在途集合（供 `cancelAl
l`、`pending` 使用）。
  3. 按编译好的链从外到内执行。每层拿到的 `ctx.request` 是外层交给它的版本。
> 4. 到达传输层：如果有层调用过 `markReplayable()`，就按类型克隆请求体（见 §8 P4）；把 `DiomanRequest` 转成 `RequestOpt
ions`，设置 `validateStatus: (_) => true` 和内核 `cancelToken`，调用 `dio.fetch`。
> 5. 工厂把响应或异常判定成 `R`，原路返回。每层可以读它、替换它，或者再次调用 `next()`。
> 6. 最外层落定：从在途集合注销、解除取消联动，然后按 §6 返回数据、返回 `R`、抛出 `R`，或抛出原始异常。
  
  ## 3. 阶段与排序
  
  | `prepare` | 请求整形，只跑一次 | 公共参数、签名前的规整 |
  | `attempt` | 重试边界；retry 以内的层每次尝试都重跑 | 每次尝试的日志、请求签名（**自定义插件的默认阶段**） |
> | `credential` | 凭证注入与鉴权失败处理 | 自定义 header 鉴权 |
  | `source` | 结果来源，可以不触网直接返回 | 本地兜底、离线数据 |
  
  规则：
  ```dart
  /// 中间件拿到的上下文；逻辑请求级字段在各层共享，request 是本层视图。
> abstract class DiomanContext<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    int get id;                                   // 逻辑请求序号
    Dioman<R> get client;
>   DiomanRequest get request;                    // 本层看到的请求，不可变
>   CancelToken get cancelToken;                  // 逻辑请求级：使用者 token ∪ cancelAll
>   bool get isCancelled;
>   DiomanLogger get log;                         // 受 debug 开关控制
    String? key();                                // 惰性计算，按 request 缓存；不可哈希或 key:false 时为 null
    T? get<T>(StateKey<T> key);                   // 逻辑请求级状态
    void set<T>(StateKey<T> key, T value);
>   R fail(DiomanFailure init);                   // 凭空造结果，必须经工厂
    void markReplayable();                        // 声明本请求可能被重发，传输层据此克隆请求体
    Future<T?> wait<T>(Future<T> pending, {Duration? timeout});  // 取消原语一，见下
>   R? cancelled();                               // 取消原语二：已取消则返回 cancel 结果
  }
  typedef DiomanNext<R> = Future<R> Function([RequestPatch? patch]);
  final class StateKey<T> { const StateKey(this.description); final String description; }
  ```
  
> **请求不可变**：`DiomanRequest` 的字段都是 final，集合是不可修改的视图。`RequestPatch` 是"要改的字段"加"要删的键"：`h
eaders`、`query`、`extra` 按键合并，`removeHeaders`、`removeQuery` 显式删除；headers 合并不区分大小写；`data`、`path`、
`method`、超时、`cancelToken` 整体替换。同一层两次调用 `next()` 拿到同一个基准请求。
  
  **取消原语**（每个 await 之后都要重查）：
> - `ctx.wait(pending, timeout:)`：等 `pending` 完成、等取消、等超时三者之一。无论哪种结局，都要取消计时器、摘掉对 toke
n 的监听，并忽略迟到的结果。调用时如果已经取消，立即返回 `null`，不挂任何监听。返回 `null` 表示被取消或超时，调用方用 `
ctx.cancelled()` 区分。
> - `ctx.cancelled()`：已取消就返回 `kind: cancel` 的结果，否则返回 `null`。
> - Dio 的 `CancelToken` 没有"摘掉监听"的接口，内核用自己的订阅登记实现 `wait`，保证测试里能断言"没有残留的监听和计时器
"（不变量 I3）。
  
  ## 5. 归一化响应
  
  ```dart
> enum DiomanKind { ok, http, biz, network, timeout, cancel, parse, auth, breaker, offline, internal }
  
  /// 归一化响应：成功、失败、被抛出的都是它。字段在构造时由工厂算好。
  class DiomanResponse<D> implements Exception {
    final DiomanRequest request; // 实际发出的请求；share/cache 时是发出那一次的
    final Response<dynamic>? raw;
>   final Object? error;         // 原始异常
    final StackTrace? errorStack;
    final StackTrace? callSite;  // 仅 debug：发起请求那一刻的调用点
  }
  | `isSuccess(code)` | `code == null \|\| code == 0 \|\| code == '0'` | 业务码判定，只在收到 2xx 响应且是信封时调用 |
  | `logicalStatus(response)` | 失败、2xx、业务码是 400–599 的整数 → 业务码；否则 httpStatus | 使用者可以把 `'TOKEN_EXP
IRED'` 映射成 401 |
> | `expectJson` | `false` | 声明 JSON 却解析失败时判为 `parse` |
  | `create(init)` | `DiomanResponse.new` | 扩展点：返回带额外字段的子类 |
  
  **分类规则**（按优先级）：
  | 输入 | kind | status |
  |---|---|---|
> | 内核 token 已取消，或 `DioExceptionType.cancel` | cancel | 0 |
  | `connectionTimeout` / `sendTimeout` / `receiveTimeout` | timeout | 0 |
  | `connectionError`、`badCertificate`，或 `unknown` 且 `error` 是 `SocketException`/`HttpException`/`TlsException` | 
network | 0 |
  | `badResponse`（只会来自使用者的 Dio 拦截器主动 reject） | http | 取响应状态 |
> | 其余 `DioException.unknown`、非 Dio 异常（使用者拦截器或 Transformer 里的 bug） | internal | 0 |
> | 2xx、`expectJson`、声明 JSON 但数据仍是非空字符串 | parse | httpStatus |
  | 收到响应 | ok / biz / http，按 `isSuccess` 与 2xx 判定 | 按 `logicalStatus` |
  
> 内核负责：工厂自己抛错时，改用内置工厂造 `internal` 结果，并保留第一次的异常，保证外层照常收尾。
  
  ## 6. 落定
  
  | 成功 | `r.data` | `r` |
  | 可预期的失败 | `throw r` | `r`（`ok == false`） |
> | 程序 bug（`kind: internal`） | `Error.throwWithStackTrace(r.error, r.errorStack)` | 同左 |
  
> - **bug 不伪装成请求失败**：中间件、判定类回调、使用者 Dio 拦截器或 Transformer 抛出的异常，链内包成 internal 交给外
层，保证 `next()` 不出错、外层的计数和注销照常完成；落定时抛出原始异常。
  - **调用点**：debug 开启时，内核在发起请求那一刻记下 `StackTrace.current`；失败落定时派生一个带 `callSite` 的副本再抛
，不修改被 share/cache 共享的原对象。生产环境不记录，零开销。
> - **`reject`**：`Dioman(reject: (r) => …)` 在抛出前转换可预期的失败，用来适配需要特定异常类型的生态。默认原样抛出 `r`
。
  
  ## 7. 插件接口
  
  ```dart
> abstract class DiomanPlugin<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    String get name;                              // 内置插件以 dioman: 开头
    DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.attempt; // 自定义插件的默认阶段
    DiomanEnforce get enforce => DiomanEnforce.post;
    DiomanPluginInstance<R> setup(Dioman<R> client);
  }
> abstract class DiomanPluginInstance<R extends DiomanResponse> {
    Future<R> handle(DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next);
    void dispose() {}
  }
  
  ```dart
> class Dioman<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
    Dioman({required Dio dio, List<DiomanPlugin<R>> plugins = const [],
            DiomanResponseFactory<R>? response, DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
>           Object Function(R r)? reject, Object? debug /* bool | DiomanLogger */,
            DiomanClock clock = const DiomanClock.system()});
    Dio get dio;
    Future<T> get<T>(String path, {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call});
    // post / put / patch / delete / head 同形；request<T>(method, path, …)
>   Future<DiomanResponse<T>> send<T>(String method, String path, {…});
    void use(DiomanPlugin<R> plugin);
    bool eject(String name);
    A? plugin<A>();                       // 取插件暴露的操作
    List<(DiomanStage, String)> inspect();
>   int cancelAll([Object? reason]);
    int get pending;
    Dioman<R> extend({BaseOptions? options, List<DiomanPlugin<R>>? plugins});
    void dispose();
  ```
  
> - `DiomanCall`：调用级配置，包含 headers、超时、使用者的 `cancelToken`、`key`、`silent`、以及各插件的请求级选项（以插
件导出的选项键为键）。
  - `extend()` 克隆 Dio 的 BaseOptions，默认继承插件列表并各自重新 `setup`，状态不共享。
> - `dispose()`：先 `cancelAll()`，再按注册的逆序调用各插件实例的 `dispose`（尽力执行完，最后抛出第一个异常）；之后拒绝
新的请求和 `use`。
  
  ## 9. 性能设计
  
  | P4 | 请求体按需克隆：只有 `markReplayable()` 时才克隆。Map/List 递归拷贝，`FormData` 用 `clone()`，`Stream` 等无法
克隆的回退原引用并在 debug 下告警，不让请求失败 |
  | P5 | 传输层 `validateStatus` 全部放行，HTTP 错误不构造 `DioException` |
> | P6 | 失败直接抛 `R`，不另建异常对象；调用点只在 debug 下记录 |
  | P7 | 入口只合并一次配置，传输层直接 `fetch`，没有二次合并 |
  | P8 | 缓存过期惰性删除，写入时按容量节流清扫（按清扫后的条目数，不按固定间隔，避免 axp B6 发现的平方级插入） |
  | P9 | 插件级配置在 `setup` 时预合并；请求级 `true` 或省略时直接复用这份配置 |
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_clock.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/fake_network.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/helpers.dart';
import '../support/pipeline/invariant_harness.dart';

/// 使用真实 Dio 转换器和假 adapter 验证传输边界。
void main() {
  test('[Dioman.cancelAll] 实际 Dio 发送中取消，迟到网络结果不会恢复请求', () async {
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final entered = Completer<void>();
    final network = FakeNetwork(onSend: (_) => entered.complete());
    network.routes['GET:/pending'] = [FakeReply(gate: gate.future)];
    final api = testClient(network: network);
    final pending = api.send<Object?>('GET', '/pending');
    await entered.future;
    expect(network.sends.length, 1);
    expect(api.cancelAll(), 1);
    expect((await pending).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect(api.pending, 0);
    gate.complete();
    await flush();
    expect(api.pending, 0);
  });
  test('[DiomanContext.markReplayable] I7 嵌套请求体经原地修改 Transformer 后每次发送一致',
      () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final clock = FakeClock();
    final harness = InvariantHarness(network, clock);
    final api = testClient(network: network, clock: clock, plugins: [
      ...harness.probes(),
      TestPlugin('replay', handler: (ctx, next) async {
        ctx.markReplayable();
        await next();
        return next();
      }),
    ]);
    api.dio.transformer = _MutatingTransformer();
    final data = {
      'items': [
        {'n': 0}
      ]
    };
    await harness.track(
        1,
        api.post<Object?>('/replay',
            data: data,
            call: const DiomanCall(headers: {invariantRequestHeader: 'one'})));
    expect(network.sends.length, 2);
    expect(network.sends.first.body, {
      'items': [
        {'n': 1}
      ]
    });
    expect(data, {
      'items': [
        {'n': 0}
      ]
    });
    harness.assertAll(api);
  });

  test('[diomanTransport] FormData 克隆、不可克隆 Stream 回退并告警、debug 日志异常隔离',
      () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final logs = <String>[];
    final api = testClient(network: network, debug: logs.add, plugins: [
      TestPlugin('replay', handler: (ctx, next) async {
        ctx.markReplayable();
        await next();
        return next();
      }),
    ]);
    final form = FormData.fromMap({'a': 'b'});
    await api.post<Object?>('/form', data: form);
    expect(form.isFinalized, isFalse);
    expect(network.sends.length, 2);
    expect(logs, isEmpty);
    final stream = Stream<List<int>>.fromIterable([
      [1, 2]
    ]).asBroadcastStream();
    final ctx = testContext(
        request: DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(
            path: '/stream', baseUrl: 'https://example.test', data: stream)),
        log: logs.add);
    ctx.markReplayable();
    await diomanTransport(api.dio, ctx);
    expect(logs, ['请求体无法克隆，回退原引用']);
    final throwLog =
        testClient(debug: (String _) => throw StateError('log'), plugins: [
      TestPlugin('log', handler: (ctx, next) {
        ctx.log('notification');
        return next();
      })
    ]);
    expect(await throwLog.get<String>('/'), 'ok');
  });

  test('[diomanTransport] 取消前不发送、传输 bug 原栈、原始 Dio 拦截器主动 reject', () async {
    final network = FakeNetwork();
    final api = testClient(network: network);
    final token = CancelToken()..cancel();
    final ctx = testContext(token: token);
    expect((await diomanTransport(api.dio, ctx)).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect(network.sends, isEmpty);
    final bug = StateError('transformer');
    api.dio.transformer = _ThrowTransformer(bug);
    await expectLater(api.post<Object?>('/bug', data: {}), throwsA(same(bug)));
    expect(api.pending, 0);
    final rejected = testClient();
    rejected.dio.interceptors.add(InterceptorsWrapper(
        onRequest: (o, h) => h.reject(DioException(
            requestOptions: o,
            type: DioExceptionType.badResponse,
            response: Response(requestOptions: o, statusCode: 418)))));
    expect((await rejected.send<Object?>('GET', '/')).status, 418);
  });
}

/// 在嵌套结构上原地改值，避免浅拷贝掩盖重放污染。
class _MutatingTransformer extends SyncTransformer {
  /// 修改后交给 Dio 的真实编码。
  @override
  Future<String> transformRequest(RequestOptions options) async {
    final item = ((options.data as Map)['items'] as List).first as Map;
    item['n'] = (item['n'] as int) + 1;
    return super.transformRequest(options);
  }
}

/// 模拟用户 Transformer 的程序 bug。
class _ThrowTransformer extends SyncTransformer {
  /// 保存原异常。
  _ThrowTransformer(this.bug);

  /// 原始异常对象。
  final Object bug;

  /// 原样抛出。
  @override
  Future<String> transformRequest(RequestOptions options) => throw bug;
}
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dioman/dioman.dart' as legacy;

/// 唯一允许同时导入新旧库的差分测试。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanKeyService.build] 固定种子 2400 组与旧 key 逐字节一致', () async {
    final random = Random(20261028);
    Object? value(int depth) {
      if (depth > 2) return random.nextInt(9);
      switch (random.nextInt(6)) {
        case 0:
          return null;
        case 1:
          return random.nextBool();
        case 2:
          return 'v${random.nextInt(30)}';
        case 3:
          return random.nextInt(100);
        case 4:
          return [value(depth + 1), value(depth + 1)];
        default:
          return {'z': value(depth + 1), 'a': value(depth + 1)};
      }
    }

    for (var i = 0; i < 2400; i++) {
      final fast = random.nextBool();
      final requestFast = random.nextBool() ? random.nextBool() : null;
      final ignores = random.nextBool() ? ['ignore'] : <String>[];
      final requestIgnores = random.nextBool() ? ['a'] : null;
      final String? scope = [null, '', 'account:$i'][i % 3];
      final String? explicit =
          i % 7 == 0 ? 'explicit:$i' : (i % 7 == 1 ? '' : null);
      String builder(RequestOptions o) => 'builder:${o.path}';
      String localBuilder(RequestOptions o) => 'request:${o.path}';
      final global = i % 11 == 0 ? builder : null;
      final local = i % 13 == 0 ? localBuilder : null;
      final request = RequestOptions(
          path: '/p${i % 15}?inline=$i',
          baseUrl: 'https://example.test',
          method: i.isEven ? 'GET' : 'POST',
          queryParameters: {'z': i, 'a': '$i', 'ignore': 1},
          data: value(0));
      final old = legacy.DiomanKey(
          fastMode: fast,
          ignores: ignores,
          builder: global,
          namespace: () => scope);
      final options = request.copyWith();
      options.extra[legacy.DiomanKey.pluginName] = legacy.DiomanKeyOptions(
          fastMode: requestFast,
          ignores: requestIgnores,
          key: explicit,
          builder: local);
      final handler = RequestInterceptorHandler();
      old.onRequest(options, handler);
      final next = DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions(
              fastMode: fast,
              ignores: ignores,
              builder: global,
              namespace: () => scope))
          .build(
              request,
              DiomanKeyOptions(
                  fastMode: requestFast,
                  ignores: requestIgnores,
                  key: explicit,
                  builder: local));
      expect(next, options.extra[legacy.kKey], reason: 'seed=20261028 case=$i');
    }
  });

  test('[DiomanKeyService.build] 开关、显式键、异常及不可序列化回退', () {
    final r = RequestOptions(path: '/r', data: {'bad': double.nan});
    const service = DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions());
    expect(service.build(r, false), isNull);
    expect(service.build(r, 'explicit'), 'explicit');
    expect(() => service.build(r, 3), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(service.build(r), contains('NaN'));
    expect(service.build(RequestOptions(data: '')), isNotNull);
    expect(service.build(RequestOptions(data: {})), isNotNull);
    final sorted = RequestOptions(data: {
      'z': {
        'b': 1,
        'a': [2, 1]
      },
      'a': 1
    });
    final reversed = RequestOptions(data: {
      'a': 1,
      'z': {
        'a': [2, 1],
        'b': 1
      }
    });
    expect(service.build(sorted), service.build(reversed));
    expect(
        () => DiomanKeyService(
                DiomanKeyOptions(builder: (_) => throw StateError('builder')))
            .build(r),
        throwsStateError);
    expect(
        () => DiomanKeyService(DiomanKeyOptions(
            namespace: () => throw StateError('namespace'))).build(r),
        throwsStateError);
  });
}

    @internal

import 'client.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';

/// 从同一基准继续执行内层；可多次调用，结果总是响应对象。
typedef DiomanNext<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    = Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> Function([RequestPatch? patch]);

/// 插件只保存配置；每个客户端 setup 一次，状态放在返回实例里。
abstract class DiomanPlugin<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 支持常量插件配置。
  const DiomanPlugin();

  /// 唯一名称；dioman: 前缀保留给内置插件。
  String get name;

  /// 默认位于尝试阶段。
  DiomanStage get stage => DiomanStage.attempt;

  /// 默认排在阶段内置插件之后。
  DiomanEnforce get enforce => DiomanEnforce.post;

  /// false 时不调用 setup，也不进 inspect。
  bool get enabled => true;

  /// 创建客户端独立状态；null 表示只有 setup 效果，不进入链。
  DiomanPluginInstance<R>? setup(Dioman<R> client);
}

/// 客户端专属插件状态及管理 API 的载体。
abstract class DiomanPluginInstance<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 执行本层，例如 `return next()`；同步和异步异常均由边界隔离。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next);

  /// 释放实例资源，客户端逆注册顺序调用。
  void dispose() {}
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'fake_clock.dart';

/// B1 启用 I1/I3/I6/I7；其余接口供后续插件批次接入。
class InvariantHarness {
  /// 绑定网络和时钟。
  InvariantHarness(this.network, this.clock);

  /// 网络发送证据。
  final FakeNetwork network;

  /// 时间资源证据。
  final FakeClock clock;

  /// 外层进入次数。
  final entries = <int, int>{};

  /// 外层离开次数。
  final exits = <int, int>{};

  /// 调用方落定次数。
  final settlements = <int, int>{};

  /// I2 回调配对序列。
  final loading = <Object, List<bool>>{};

  /// I4 每请求通知次数。
  final notifications = <int, int>{};

  /// I5 每轮刷新次数。
  final refreshRounds = <int, int>{};

  /// 各层保存的上下文，用于请求结束后追踪取消订阅。
  final contexts = <DiomanContext<Result>>[];

  /// 生成最外层及每阶段 pre/post 的直通探针。
  List<DiomanPlugin<Result>> probes() => [
        TestPlugin('invariant',
            stage: DiomanStage.outer,
            enforce: DiomanEnforce.pre, handler: (ctx, next) async {
          entries.update(ctx.id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
          contexts.add(ctx);
          final r = await next();
          exits.update(ctx.id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
          return r;
        }),
        for (final stage in DiomanStage.values)
          for (final enforce in DiomanEnforce.values)
            TestPlugin('probe:${stage.name}:${enforce.name}',
                stage: stage, enforce: enforce, handler: (ctx, next) async {
              final r = await next();
              expect(r.kind, isNot(DiomanKind.internal),
                  reason: 'I6 next 不抛且受控流程无 internal');
              return r;
            }),
      ];

  /// 登记一个逻辑调用及其最终一次落定。
  Future<T> track<T>(int id, Future<T> pending) => pending.whenComplete(() {
        settlements.update(id, (n) => n + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
      });

  /// 统一断言不变量；测试夹具自身的坏证据也必须被拒绝。
  void assertAll(Dioman<Result> api) {
    expect(entries, isNotEmpty, reason: 'I1 不允许空跑');
    expect(entries.keys.toSet(), exits.keys.toSet());
    expect(entries.keys.toSet(), settlements.keys.toSet());
    for (final id in entries.keys) {
      expect([entries[id], exits[id], settlements[id]], [1, 1, 1]);
    }
    expect(api.pending, 0, reason: 'I3');
    expect(api.cancellationSubscriptions, 0);
    expect(contexts.every((c) => c.cancellationSubscriptions == 0), isTrue);
    expect(clock.pendingTimers, 0);
    final bodies = <Object?, String>{};
    for (final send in network.sends) {
      if (send.invariant == null) continue;
      final body = jsonEncode(send.body);
      expect(body, bodies.putIfAbsent(send.invariant, () => body),
          reason: 'I7');
    }
    for (final sequence in loading.values) {
      expect(sequence.length.isEven, isTrue);
      for (var i = 0; i < sequence.length; i++) {
        expect(sequence[i], i.isEven);
      }
    }
    expect(notifications.values.every((n) => n <= 1), isTrue);
    expect(refreshRounds.values.every((n) => n == 1), isTrue);
  }
}

/// 有效 Dio 配置的不可变请求视图；入口合并一次后构造。
class DiomanRequest {
  /// 从 [options] 拷贝有效配置；[call] 保存调用选项，例如 `DiomanRequest(ro)`。
  DiomanRequest(RequestOptions options, {DiomanCall call = const DiomanCall()})
      : _options = options.copyWith(),
        data = options.data,
        headers = Map.unmodifiable(options.headers),
        query = Map.unmodifiable(options.queryParameters),
        extra = Map.unmodifiable(options.extra),
        call = DiomanCall(
            key: call.key,
            envelope: call.envelope,
            silent: call.silent,
            options: Map.unmodifiable(call.options));

  final RequestOptions _options;

  final Object? data;

  /// 不可修改的请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic> headers;

/// The internal request option class that is the eventual result after
/// [BaseOptions] and [Options] are composed.
class RequestOptions extends _RequestConfig with OptionsMixin {
  RequestOptions({
    this.path = '',
    this.data,
    this.onReceiveProgress,
    this.onSendProgress,
    this.cancelToken,
    super.method,
    super.sendTimeout,
    super.receiveTimeout,
    super.transformTimeout,
    Duration? connectTimeout,
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
    String? baseUrl,
    super.extra,
    super.headers,
    super.preserveHeaderCase,
    super.responseType,
    super.contentType,
    super.validateStatus,
    super.receiveDataWhenStatusError,
    super.followRedirects,
    super.maxRedirects,
    super.persistentConnection,
    super.requestEncoder,
    super.responseDecoder,
    super.listFormat,
    bool? setRequestContentTypeWhenNoPayload,
    StackTrace? sourceStackTrace,
  }) : assert(connectTimeout == null || !connectTimeout.isNegative) {
    this.sourceStackTrace = sourceStackTrace ?? StackTrace.current;
    this.queryParameters = queryParameters ?? {};
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl ?? '';
    this.connectTimeout = connectTimeout;
  }

  /// Create a [RequestOptions] from current instance with merged attributes.
  RequestOptions copyWith({
    String? method,
    Duration? sendTimeout,
    Duration? receiveTimeout,
    Duration? transformTimeout,
    Duration? connectTimeout,
    dynamic data,
    String? path,
    Map<String, dynamic>? queryParameters,
    String? baseUrl,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    Map<String, dynamic>? extra,
    Map<String, dynamic>? headers,
    bool? preserveHeaderCase,
    ResponseType? responseType,
    String? contentType,
    ValidateStatus? validateStatus,
    bool? receiveDataWhenStatusError,
    bool? followRedirects,
    int? maxRedirects,
    bool? persistentConnection,
    RequestEncoder? requestEncoder,
    ResponseDecoder? responseDecoder,
    ListFormat? listFormat,
    bool? setRequestContentTypeWhenNoPayload,
  }) {
    final contentTypeInHeader = headers != null &&
        headers.keys
            .map((e) => e.toLowerCase())
            .contains(Headers.contentTypeHeader);

    assert(
      !(contentType != null && contentTypeInHeader),
      'You cannot set both contentType param and a content-type header',
    );

    final ro = RequestOptions(
      method: method ?? this.method,
      sendTimeout: sendTimeout ?? this.sendTimeout,
      receiveTimeout: receiveTimeout ?? this.receiveTimeout,
      transformTimeout: transformTimeout ?? this.transformTimeout,
      connectTimeout: connectTimeout ?? this.connectTimeout,
      data: data ?? this.data,
      path: path ?? this.path,
      baseUrl: baseUrl ?? this.baseUrl,
      queryParameters: queryParameters ?? Map.from(this.queryParameters),
      onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress ?? this.onReceiveProgress,
      onSendProgress: onSendProgress ?? this.onSendProgress,
      cancelToken: cancelToken ?? this.cancelToken,
      extra: extra ?? Map.from(this.extra),
      headers: headers ?? Map.from(this.headers),
      preserveHeaderCase: preserveHeaderCase ?? this.preserveHeaderCase,
      responseType: responseType ?? this.responseType,
      validateStatus: validateStatus ?? this.validateStatus,
      receiveDataWhenStatusError:
          receiveDataWhenStatusError ?? this.receiveDataWhenStatusError,
      followRedirects: followRedirects ?? this.followRedirects,
      maxRedirects: maxRedirects ?? this.maxRedirects,
      persistentConnection: persistentConnection ?? this.persistentConnection,
      requestEncoder: requestEncoder ?? this.requestEncoder,
      responseDecoder: responseDecoder ?? this.responseDecoder,
      listFormat: listFormat ?? this.listFormat,
      sourceStackTrace: sourceStackTrace,
    );

    if (contentType != null) {
      ro.headers.remove(Headers.contentTypeHeader);
      ro.contentType = contentType;
    } else if (!contentTypeInHeader) {
      ro.contentType = this.contentType;
    }

    return ro;
  }

  /// The source [StackTrace] which should always point to the invocation of
  /// [DioMixin.request] or if not provided, to the construction of the
  /// [RequestOptions] instance. In both instances the source context should
  /// still be available before it is lost due to asynchronous operations.
  @internal
  StackTrace? sourceStackTrace;

  /// Generate the requesting [Uri] from the options.
  Uri get uri {
    String url = path;
    if (!url.startsWith(RegExp(r'https?:'))) {
      url = baseUrl + url;
      final s = url.split(':/');
      if (s.length == 2) {
        url = '${s[0]}:/${s[1].replaceAll('//', '/')}';
      }
    }
    final query = Transformer.urlEncodeQueryMap(queryParameters, listFormat);

## 约束

- 改 `lib/src/` 前必读：`.claude/skills/dioman/SKILL.md`
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

/// 请求补丁及统一请求选项解析的规则测试。
void main() {
  test('[RequestPatch] 合并、大小写删除、null 替换与完整有效配置保留', () {
    final token = CancelToken();
    final original = RequestOptions(
        path: '/old',
        baseUrl: 'https://example.test',
        method: 'POST',
        data: {'x': 1},
        headers: {'X-A': 1, 'X-B': 2},
        queryParameters: {'a': 1, 'b': 2},
        extra: {'a': 1},
        connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 1),
        sendTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 2),
        receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 3),
        cancelToken: token,
        followRedirects: false,
        maxRedirects: 7,
        responseType: ResponseType.bytes,
        persistentConnection: false,
        listFormat: ListFormat.csv,
        onSendProgress: (_, __) {},
        onReceiveProgress: (_, __) {});
    final optionKey = DiomanOptionKey<int>('n');
    final r = DiomanRequest(original,
        call: DiomanCall(silent: true, options: {optionKey: 3}));
    final p = r.patch(const RequestPatch(
        headers: {'x-a': 4, 'Null': null},
        removeHeaders: {'x-b'},
        query: {'b': null, 'c': 3},
        removeQuery: {'a'},
        extra: {'b': 2},
        data: null,
        method: 'PUT',
        path: '/new',
        connectTimeout: null,
        sendTimeout: null,
        receiveTimeout: null,
        cancelToken: null));
    expect(p.headers, {'x-a': 4, 'Null': null});
    expect(p.query, {'b': null, 'c': 3});
    expect(p.extra, {'a': 1, 'b': 2});
    expect(p.data, isNull);
    expect([p.path, p.method, p.baseUrl], ['/new', 'PUT', original.baseUrl]);
    expect([p.connectTimeout, p.sendTimeout, p.receiveTimeout, p.cancelToken],
        everyElement(isNull));
    expect(r.headers['X-A'], 1);
    expect(r.cancelToken, same(token));
    expect(r.silent, isTrue);
    expect(r.call.options[optionKey], 3);
    expect(() => r.headers['x'] = 1, throwsUnsupportedError);
    expect(() => r.query['x'] = 1, throwsUnsupportedError);
    expect(() => r.extra['x'] = 1, throwsUnsupportedError);
    expect(() => r.call.options[optionKey] = 1, throwsUnsupportedError);
    final unchanged = r.patch(const RequestPatch());
    expect(unchanged.data, same(r.data));
    expect(unchanged.uri, r.uri);
    final wire = p.toRequestOptions();
    expect(wire.followRedirects, isFalse);
    expect(wire.responseType, ResponseType.bytes);
    expect(wire.maxRedirects, 7);
    expect(wire.onSendProgress, same(original.onSendProgress));
    expect(wire.onReceiveProgress, same(original.onReceiveProgress));
    wire.headers.clear();
    expect(p.headers, isNotEmpty);
    final replacement = CancelToken();
    final changed = r.patch(RequestPatch(
        data: [2],
        cancelToken: replacement,
        connectTimeout: Duration.zero,
        sendTimeout: Duration.zero,
        receiveTimeout: Duration.zero));
    expect(changed.cancelToken, same(replacement));
    expect(changed.data, [2]);
    expect(
        [changed.connectTimeout, changed.sendTimeout, changed.receiveTimeout],
        everyElement(Duration.zero));
  });

  test('[resolveOption] 四种写法、简写、optIn 和未知类型', () {
    final base = <int>[1];
    List<int> merge(List<int> a, List<int> b) => [...a, ...b];
    for (final value in [null, true]) {
      expect(resolveOption<List<int>>(value, base: base, merge: merge),
          same(base));
    }
    expect(resolveOption<List<int>>(false, base: base, merge: merge), isNull);
    expect(
        resolveOption<List<int>>(null, base: base, merge: merge, optIn: true),
        isNull);
    expect(
        resolveOption<List<int>>(true, base: base, merge: merge, optIn: true),
        same(base));
    expect(resolveOption<List<int>>([2], base: base, merge: merge), [1, 2]);
    expect(
        resolveOption<List<int>>(3,
            base: base, merge: merge, shorthand: (v) => v is int ? [v] : null),
        [1, 3]);
    expect(() => resolveOption<List<int>>('bad', base: base, merge: merge),
        throwsArgumentError);
    expect(
        () => resolveOption<List<int>>('bad',
            base: base, merge: merge, shorthand: (_) => null),
        throwsArgumentError);
    expect(identical(DiomanOptionKey<int>('x'), DiomanOptionKey<int>('x')),
        isFalse);
    expect(DiomanOptionKey<int>('x').description, 'x');
  });
}
